- **R4 parts:** sysinfo 0.39.x `system`,`disk` (MIT, adopt); hex `docs/plans/language-recommendations.md` table (MIT, data); TypeWhisper `SetupWizardEngineRecommendation` tests (GPL-3.0, port as spec); MacParakeet silent locale rule (GPL, port idea).
- **Handy files touched:** `catalog/mod.rs` (stale `HANDY_RANKING` bypassed via the overlay; RE-15); `tray.rs` (Model ▸ and Unload removed through `fork::tray::extend`).
- **New fork files:** `fork/models/{policy,machine,upgrade,cleanup}.rs`, `src/fork/settings/advanced/Models.tsx` (wraps Handy's model page).
- **Tests:** policy unit tests (≥ 60 vectors across languages, RAM, disk, chip, measured speed, both DEC-26 tables); swap never during a take; old model deleted only after the new one loaded; retired-model migration.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** RS-09 policy part on the real slice: language change to ja+en re-picks; Intel picks CPU (DEC-29).
- **Exit criteria:** policy table matches RS-09 on each machine class; no model name visible outside Advanced.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-07a, RS-09.
- **As built (2026-10-31), decisions that refine this block** (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Silent model policy, upgrades and cleanup (S-07b)"; procedure in `docs/fork/MAC-TEST-SCRIPT.md` S-07b; run `docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-S-07b.md`): (1) **Policy data** in `fork/models/policy.fork.json`, not code: RS-09's short-take p50/p95 (M5 Max, Metal; CPU only where RS-09 measured it), peak footprints, rankings per language with and without CC-BY (both DEC-26 tables; `*` for languages RS-09 did not measure), and **machine-class factors** (M1-M5 × base/Pro/Max/Ultra relative to the M5 Max; base M1 6×, Pro M2 2.5×, Max M5 1×). The factors are our estimates until the RS-09 M1 8 GB / M 16 GB runs; the **measured factor** (median key-up of 5 warm 2-20 s takes over RS-09's p50, `fork.db` › `asr_speed`, no text) replaces the class factor when it is slower, never when faster (a lucky measurement never starts a bigger download). RAM tiers cap the peak footprint (8 GB: 1,400 MB, so Parakeet 0.6B fits and the §5.2 LLM-skip rule covers the rest; 16 GB: 3,000 MB). Disk: file + 5 GB on sysinfo's purgeable-aware free space, a file already on disk does not count. (2) **Resulting picks** (vectors in `policy_tests.rs`, 59 table rows plus 15 rule tests): English → parakeet-unified-en-0.6b on every Apple Silicon class (RS-09's English main pick; turbo's edge is within one speaker's noise); nl/fr/de/es → turbo on M3-M5 Max and the Ultras, Parakeet TDT 0.6B v3 elsewhere; ja/zh → turbo or whisper-small; en + nl → turbo or Parakeet v3; without CC-BY the Whisper ladder; Intel (DEC-29, not built in M1) only models with CPU numbers (Parakeet v3, whisper-base) against the Intel budget; nothing fast enough → the fastest that fits and one "slower on this Mac" inbox entry (`NotificationKind::SlowerMac`, inbox only). DEC-02's "OS languages + English" applies when the user has not chosen languages; chosen languages are used as they are. (3) **Slow links (S-07a open item a):** the English starter is now parakeet-110m **Q4_K_M** (90 MB, 72 s at 10 Mbit/s) on every link; the RS-09 bench on the smaller quants gave 8.0 % vs 6.5 % WER on short takes (+2 errors in 138 words) and 5.7 % vs 6.1 % on the 203 s take. It is not a catalog default, so Handy's downloader cannot fetch it: `setup::transfer_alternate` fetches it with hf-hub into the same cache at the pinned revision (no token; debug builds follow a loopback `HF_ENDPOINT` like the probe), Handy's rescan registers it, and S-07a's checksum and retry machine are unchanged. whisper-base stays Q8_0 (85 MB, 68 s; its Q4_K_M is slower on Metal and worse in French). (4) **Upgrades** run inside S-07a's setup loop (`upgrade::step`), triggered by launch, the first model, a language change, 5 more timed takes, a Handy download/selection event and "Choose automatically". The background download is S-07a's state machine in `Mode::Background` (progress to the Models panel only, no bar, a stop rests 30 min instead of waiting for a Retry the user cannot see). The swap waits until nothing records or transcribes and no take ended for 10 s, then `switch_active_model` loads the model; a load failure keeps the old selection and skips that pick for the session. Handy's "Dictation is ready" notification is posted only for the first model. (5) **Cleanup** (`fork.db` › `speech_models`, migration 0007): a superseded `starter`/`policy` model, or a retired selection the fork migrated away from, is deleted only after the new selection loaded; a model the user downloaded on the Models page is never deleted; Handy's retired ONNX files go once a working model is selected. The policy's own `retired` list (`whisper-large`, `whisper-large-v2`) is hidden on the Models page (with the DEC-27 licence filter) and a selection of one migrates. (6) **The user's choice pins:** a selection that is not the fork's while the fork's model is still on disk is the user's (Models page) and turns "Choose the model automatically" off; turning it on again re-picks. (7) **Language changes:** Handy's `change_spoken_languages_setting` is observed in `fork::compose_invoke` (the fork's own `lib.rs` hook), so no Handy file changes; onboarding keeps calling `fork_speech_setup_refresh`. (8) **No model name outside Advanced:** the tray's Model ▸ submenu, Unload and the doubled separator are removed in the existing `fork::tray::extend` hook (no new `tray.rs` line); Handy's onboarding All set panel gets no model when the fork manages it (one more `OnboardingFlow.tsx` expression, 5 changed lines after prettier, budget 8 → 13; `Cargo.toml` budget 20 → 21 for sysinfo) and its empty Model row is hidden by `src/fork/onboarding/firstRun.css`; S-29a replaces the flow. (9) **Settings › Advanced › Speech models** (`src/fork/settings/advanced/Models.tsx`, debug panel until S-14): model in use, background phase and progress, slower note, this Mac and the languages, "Choose the model automatically", and Handy's Models page folded under "All models" (wrapped, retired and restricted models pruned from Handy's model store). (10) **R4 parts:** sysinfo 0.39 (`system`, `disk`) adopted; hex's language table, TypeWhisper's recommendation tests and MacParakeet's locale rule were not used (RS-09's own table is the data; the vectors are ours), so no PORTS row. **Real-Mac run:** (M-26, test identity, local Hugging Face stand-in): English from an empty profile: Q4_K_M starter ready 13 s after launch, the policy picked parakeet-unified-en, downloaded it in the background, swapped it in 12 s after a take (transcribed by the starter in 230 ms) and deleted the starter; at 10 Mbit/s the starter was ready in **78 s** (§5.3 met; S-07a: 134 s) and the 731 MB upgrade followed in 10 min with dictation working meanwhile; relaunch with ja + en re-picked whisper-large-v3-turbo, swapped it and deleted unified; tray model items removed; one "Dictation is ready" for a whole first run. **Not verified:** the Speech models panel and pinning by a click in the real app (another agent's window held the front; Playwright covers the panel), a live language change in Settings (relaunch stands in; the observed command is unit-tested), Intel (no x86_64 build), M1 8 GB / M 16 GB (class factors are estimates), the measured-speed step-down and "slower on this Mac" on a real slow Mac, a full disk, the All set panel by eye, real Hugging Face for the Q4_K_M file. **Mac wave 1 fix (`fix/wave1-a`, 2026-11-03), hysteresis:** under load 60-96 the measured factor (18.2) flipped the pick between parakeet-110m and unified-en, re-downloading 135-731 MB per cycle, because the factor was per model (the light model had no takes, so the class factor picked the big one again) and busy takes counted. Decisions: a take is not recorded when the 1-min load per core is above 0.75; a factor counts only from two sets of 5 takes, both slower than the class, spread over ≥ 30 min, ≤ 14 days old (the faster set's median); a confirmed slow factor is the Mac's, saved in `model_policy_state` › `measured_factor` for 14 days and used while the new selection has no confirmed factor (a fast reading does not clear it early); a model the fork deleted is not downloaded again for 7 days while the selection still hears the user's languages; every policy round waits for a quiet moment before any model work and the checksum runs at background QoS. No migration (existing tables). Tests: `speed::tests::{a_busy_mac_is_not_measured,a_factor_needs_two_slow_sets_over_half_an_hour}`, `upgrade::tests::{a_slow_verdict_outlives_the_swap_it_caused,a_deleted_model_is_not_downloaded_again_during_the_cooldown,the_load_spike_of_mac_wave_1_no_longer_loops}`. The 17 s paste delay's exact cause was not proven from the log (the paste path shares no lock with model work); queued: MAC-TEST-SCRIPT S-07b.8 (`scripts/wb-s07b/loop.sh`, no audio) and S-07b.9 (one take under load).

### S-08 Microphone and sound polish

- **Goal:** the right mic by default and honest audio feedback.
- **User-visible outcome:** mic list in this order: the built-in mic marked "(recommended)", "Auto-detect (system setting)", then other devices, with a caution on Bluetooth devices ("Wired or built-in mics work best; Bluetooth can drop out"). If the system default is a Bluetooth headset and the user never chose, the built-in mic is used. Unplugging a mic mid-take keeps what was heard. If Microphone permission is revoked while running, macOS lets the take continue until the app quits (RS-11, DEC-55): the take finishes normally; if the user picks Quit & Reopen, the take's audio is saved first and offered after relaunch; the next take that cannot open the mic shows the pill error with Fix. Sounds are on by default with Handy's themes. "Mute all audio while dictating" uses native CoreAudio (off by default). A level-meter API powers the mic test.
- **R2 rows closed:** A-37, A-38, A-40, A-42, A-211
- **Regression protected (HAVE):** A-39, A-41, A-43, A-44
- **R4 parts:** handy-recorder `backend/headset_macos.rs` transport type (MIT, port ~40 lines); Tambourine `audio_mute/macos.rs` + `tests/audio_mute_tests.rs` (AGPL-3.0, port; file marked AGPL; W-29).
- **Handy files touched:** `managers/audio.rs:100-111` and `:204` (both `osascript` calls delegate to `fork::audio`), `commands/audio.rs` (device list labels through a fork wrapper command, no edit).
- **New fork files:** `fork/audio/{mod,transport,mute,monitor,permission}.rs`, `src/fork/settings/MicrophonePicker.tsx`.
- **Tests:** transport classification; default-mic rule; list order; mute save/restore per device and `get_mute` without `osascript`; monitor mode does not create history rows; permission-revoke handling with a fake.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** RS-11 mic-loss and permission-revoke cases (DEC-55: revoke mid-take, then Quit & Reopen keeps the audio; a real USB unplug and AirPods drop, which RS-11 could only simulate); AirPods connected as default → built-in used; mute restores the previous volume exactly.
- **Exit criteria:** no `osascript` spawn anywhere in a take or at mute; mic loss keeps partial text.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-03, RS-11.
- **As built (2026-10-31), decisions that refine this block** (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Microphone and sounds (S-08)"; real-Mac evidence in `docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-S-08.md`): (1) **Files:** `fork/audio/{transport,choice,watch,monitor,mute,mute_tests,permission}.rs` (`choice.rs` holds the pure rules, `watch.rs` the device thread, `mute_tests.rs` the ported Tambourine tests), `fork/commands/audio.rs`, `src/fork/settings/{MicrophonePicker,SoundSettings}.tsx` on the debug panel until S-14. (2) **"Never chosen" vs "Auto-detect"**: Handy's `selected_microphone = None` plus a fork setting `mic_follow_system` (written only by `fork_mic_choose`). Never chosen = the system default except that a **wireless** default (Bluetooth/LE, an iPhone _over the air_, AirPlay) is replaced by the built-in mic when there is one; "Auto-detect (system setting)" follows the default even when it is Bluetooth. Unset is shown as Auto-detect selected, with a note naming the skipped headset and a "Use … anyway" button (= Auto-detect). (3) The rule runs **inside the existing `recorder_config` hook** (no new hook): `device: None` becomes `coreaudio:<built-in UID>`; the hook also records the opened device for the bar's mic label and the watch. (4) List order: built-in "(recommended)", Auto-detect (with "Now: <default>"), then the others **wired before wireless**; wireless rows carry a "Bluetooth" tag and the list the caution. (5) **Mic lost mid-take:** the take is not stopped for the user; a 5 Hz HAL watch during takes shows the bar warning "Mic lost, keeping what you said" (`PillWarning::MicLost`, outranks "stops soon") and Handy keeps the audio before the loss; at rest the watch re-prepares a stale prepared mic (Auto-detect default change, vanished device), never in a take, not within 2 s of a press. (6) **Mute** is a Tambourine port in an AGPL file; Handy's two macOS `osascript` bodies are **deleted** and call `fork::audio::mute::{set_mute,get_mute}` (`managers/audio.rs` budget 4 → 26); a session is restored on the device that was muted; a user mute is left alone and re-checked at the next take; restore at `RunEvent::Exit`. (7) **DEC-55:** the take running at quit is saved at `ExitRequested` (and `Exit`) to `<data>/recovered/` and handed at the next launch to Handy's file transcription (`TranscriptionJobManager::transcribe_file`), inbox kind `take_recovered`; the file is removed once History has it. (8) **Level meter:** `fork_mic_monitor_start/stop` + `ForkMicLevel` (20 Hz, RMS on −60..0 dBFS), its own handy-recorder, ends on stop, 60 s or a take start; never a take. (9) **Test identities** (`com.wisprbro.<x>check`) run the product runtime (S-07a made `is_product_build()` true for them; S-08 needed the same for its Mac run and keeps a test of the rule), so a Mac test bundle with its own TCC identity runs the prepared mic, hotkey engine, bar and watch. (10) Sounds stay Handy's own Marimba/Pop files (on by default via F-07); no sound file added. **Real-Mac run** (M5 Max, macOS 26.6.2, test bundle `com.wisprbro.s08check` launched with `open`): mute 1 during the take and mute flag + volume bits identical after, user mute kept, no `osascript`; stand-in mic loss (public aggregate over the built-in mic) detected by the watch in the same second (Handy's recorder reported `DeviceLost` only at the stop, 4 s later), the words before the loss in History, re-prepared on the built-in mic; `tccutil reset` mid-take did not stop the take (165 s of audio) and **showed no Quit & Reopen dialog** on this run, so the quit was sent as the same Apple event: 29.5 s saved at quit, in History after the relaunch with the inbox entry; Don't Allow in the same process gives "Only silence came in" (the open succeeds, silent; RS-11's stale status), after a relaunch "Microphone access is off" with Fix. **Not verified:** a real USB unplug and AirPods drop, AirPods as default (no Bluetooth hardware; unit and Playwright tests), the Quit & Reopen button itself, the mic-lost warning and the Settings panel and meter by eye in the real app (Playwright only), VoiceOver.
- **Owner test 1 fix (2026-11-03, `fix/owner-test-1`):** the meter read dBFS on a fixed -60..0 scale, so a café's -30 dBFS hum sat at half and speech moved it one bar. `fork/audio/dynamics.rs`: RMS of the last 30 ms in dBFS, minus a noise floor (15th percentile of 1.5 s; 4 dB lower during the first 0.6 s), a 4.5 dB dead band, a 20 dB span with an ease-out curve, 40 ms attack / 200 ms release from the audio's own time; 30 events a second carrying the highest level since the last. `settings/meterBars.ts`: no bar below 0.04, lit bars vary in height per reading (not with Reduce Motion). Offline replay of the café WAV through the sink (`meter_sequence_of_a_wav`, `scripts/wb-meter/make-wav.py`): noise 1-4 s mean 0.01 (0.1 bars), speech mean 0.41 / max 0.81 (up to 10 bars, 3-10 moving), noise after mean 0.01, silence 0. Unit tests on synthetic sequences (`dynamics.rs`, `monitor.rs`, `meterBars.test.ts`), Playwright light/dark/Reduce Motion states (`microphone.spec.ts`).

### Data and text intelligence foundations

### S-09 History data model, retention, stats store and app capture

- **Goal:** the data every Hub feature needs, stored once and correctly.
- **User-visible outcome:** History keeps everything (text forever, audio 14 days by default); stats survive retention and deletes; each entry knows its app; a full disk during recording keeps the text.
- **R2 rows closed:** C-46, C-212, D-06, D-21
- **R4 parts:** rusqlite + rusqlite_migration (in tree); unicode-segmentation 1.12 (MIT/Apache, adopt direct) + CJK/Thai rule (W-14); objc2-app-kit `NSWorkspace` (in tree) for the frontmost app; SpeakoFlow `insights.rs` and Muesli `DictationStore.swift` stats patterns (MIT, port); AudioWhisper / OpenWhispr bundle-id → category map (MIT, data).
- **Handy files touched:** ⚠ `managers/history.rs` (save/delete/retry hooks); `managers/transcription.rs:1600` (returns verbatim ASR text through the S-11 hook); ⚠ `actions.rs` (`on_record_start` captures frontmost app; `audio_ms` passed at save, `actions.rs:786-793`).
- **New fork files:** `fork/db/migrations/0002_history.sql`, `fork/db/{entry_meta,stats,fts,retention,reconcile}.rs`, `fork/stats/{wordcount,streak,wpm}.rs`, `fork/context/frontmost.rs`, `fork/context/categories.json` (`style_group`, `usage_category`, `is_ai_chat` per app, §4.3).
- **Rules:** count each non-empty dictation once; a retry updates counts by the delta; user deletes never subtract; file, practice and failed takes are excluded (C-48, C-211); local day at record time; `paste_outcome` from `deliver()` stored per take; retention job at launch and every 6 h deletes WAVs past the audio limit and rows past the text limit (Handy rows through Handy's history manager API), never stats; soft delete with a 10 s purge delay; WAV names include milliseconds (RE-35); launch reconcile per §4.3; disk full while recording → recording stops with a pill message, transcript delivered from memory to the card and an inbox entry (from S-18).
- **Tests:** migration tests; word counts (en, de, ja, zh, th, mixed); WPM on speech ms; retention with a fake clock; stats invariants across retry/delete; FTS search incl. < 3 chars fallback; Handy history rows deleted only through the manager API; kill between the Handy write and the fork write, relaunch → `entry_meta` backfilled; orphan `entry_meta` dropped; taxonomy table tests; disk-full fake.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** 50 takes in 5 apps produce correct per-app rows; 14-day audio expiry simulated by clock override.
- **Exit criteria:** all invariants tested; `paste_outcome` recorded per take; a 10,000-row history searches in ≤ 100 ms.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / F-06, S-04.
- **As built (2026-10-30), decisions that change the plan above:**
  - **Hooks:** `managers/history.rs` 3 lines (`on_saved` in `insert_entry`, `on_retried` in `update_transcription`, `on_deleted` in `delete_entry`; Handy's `MIGRATIONS` untouched); `actions.rs` +3 (`fork::on_record_start(app)`, `fork::take_file_name(sample_count)` replacing F-01's name call, `if wav_saved || fork::keep_take(..)`); `managers/transcription.rs` 6 (`finish_text` hands the verbatim text to `fork::note_verbatim`; S-11 replaces it). No `save_entry` signature change: speech time and app reach the row through `fork::context::take::Ledger`, keyed by the WAV file name (bound at stop) and the kept text (verbatim), never "the latest take". Budgets in `ci/hooks.txt`.
  - **Files:** as planned, plus `fork/db/{handy,jobs,disk}.rs` (the only path to Handy's history; the jobs thread; full disk), `fork/context/take.rs` (the ledger), `fork/commands/history.rs` (`fork_stats_summary`, `fork_history_search`, `fork_history_meta`, `fork_history_delete`, `fork_history_undo_delete`; S-15a, S-15b and S-25 build on them) and the `ForkStatsUpdated` event. FTS stayed in F-06's `fork/db/fts.rs`; `fork/stats/` is pure math and `fork/db/stats.rs` writes the tables.
  - **Schema (0002):** `entry_meta.day`, `audio_file`, `counted`; `daily_stats.timed_words` (WPM numerator: words of rows with known speech time, so backfilled rows without audio cannot inflate WPM; SpeakoFlow pattern).
  - **Counting:** the first write fixes time, day, app, taxonomy, source and speech time; later writes move the stats by the delta (a failed take fixed by a retry is counted then; a retry that comes back empty uncounts it). Only `source = dictation` with non-empty final text counts. Deletes (user, retention, orphan drop) never subtract.
  - **Retention:** audio past its limit = plain WAV delete + `audio_deleted_at`; text past its limit = `HistoryManager::delete_entry`. Never touched: stats, files the user transcribed (Handy's own rule), rows Handy marks `saved`. Runs at launch after the reconcile, every 6 h and on a retention setting change. Debug builds read `WB_DEBUG_RETENTION_CLOCK_OFFSET_DAYS` for the clock-override test.
  - **Word counts:** UAX #29 words; Han/Kana one per character; Thai/Lao/Khmer/Myanmar grapheme clusters per run ÷ 4 (rounded, at least 1), summed per run because `unicode-segmentation` splits a Thai run into many segments; Hangul counts like English.
  - **Taxonomy:** `categories.json` = OpenWhispr's whole-token signatures + AudioWhisper bundle ids (both MIT) + our documents and AI apps. ChatGPT.app's current bundle id is `com.openai.codex` (found on the test Mac), listed beside `com.openai.chat`. Cursor and Windsurf are `ai_prompts` but not `is_ai_chat`; browsers stay `other` until S-19b.
  - **Full disk:** the record-start hook starts a watcher (statvfs every 1 s while recording); under 50 MB free it stops the recording through S-04's session path (`Interruption::DiskFull` → `CardReason::DiskFull`, string `insert.cardReason.diskFull`). If the WAV still cannot be saved, `keep_take` keeps the history row text-only (`audio_deleted_at` set). The pill message (S-03) and the inbox entry (S-18) are log lines until those slices.
  - **Soft delete:** `deleted_at` hides the row from search at once; purge through Handy after 10 s; the launch reconcile finishes purges a quit cut short.
  - **Fix found on the way (F-06):** `fork.db` now follows portable mode (`Data/`), next to Handy's `history.db`; before, it used the normal app data dir even in portable mode.
  - **Real-Mac result (2026-10-30, M5 Max, macOS 26.6.2; `docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-S-09.md`):** in an isolated portable debug build, 52 takes through the real record path into TextEdit, Notes, Mail, Messages and Claude: 52/52 rows with the right app and taxonomy, `history.db` and `entry_meta` ids equal, stats exactly equal to the counted rows (289 words, 46 dictations, 385,586 speech ms; 6 near-silent takes not counted), `paste_outcome` on every delivered take, and 2/2 `confirmed` with ⌘V into TextEdit (first run of S-04's delivery inside the app). Clock override 15 days: 51/51 WAVs deleted, rows, text and stats unchanged; 13 days: nothing deleted. A soft delete left by a quit was purged at launch through Handy's API. 10,000-row search: ≤ 7 ms per query in a debug build (budget 100 ms). Not run: the disk-full case on a real disk (unit tests only), the undo UI (S-15b).

### S-10 Local LLM runtime

- **Goal:** a bundled, supervised, private LLM ready for every text feature.
- **User-visible outcome:** after the speech model, the app silently downloads the right cleanup model; Settings › Advanced › "AI engine" (on the fork debug panel until S-14) shows Ready and a "Try it" button that cleans a sample sentence.
- **R2 rows closed:** D-67, D-71, D-211, D-212, D-213
- **Regression protected (HAVE):** E-82
- **R4 parts:** llama.cpp `llama-server` pinned tag (MIT, vendor-build, W-04); TypeWhisper `LlamaCppServer.swift`, `LlamaCppRuntime.swift`, `LlamaCppPluginTests.swift` (GPL-3.0-or-later, port; tests as spec; never its runtime download); SpeakoFlow `managers/local_llm.rs` prewarm/resident ideas (MIT, port parts); reqwest 0.12.28 `unix_socket` (in tree); Qwen3.5-2B Q4_K_M and Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 Q4_K_M (Apache-2.0, mirrored to our HF org, pinned revision + sha256; DEC-34); Handy hf-hub downloader (keep).
- **Handy files touched:** ⚠ `settings.rs` (`local` provider via the providers hook); `llm_client.rs` (`RequestOptions`: transport, temperature, max_tokens, timeout, neutral headers; upstream-bound); `managers/model.rs` (`is_fork_asset` so our GGUFs are not treated as ASR); `wb-build.yml` builds the binary.
- **New fork files:** `fork/llm/{supervisor,process,transport,health,memory,catalog,download}.rs`, `ci/llama-server/build.sh`, `src/fork/settings/advanced/AiEngine.tsx`; the `llama-server` backend for `scripts/wb-translate-missing.ts`.
- **Behaviour:** the supervisor starts or wakes the sidecar at key-down with the ordered loads of §4.5 (prewarm, D-211) so model load hides inside recording time; it alone stops the sidecar after 120 s idle; on wake from sleep it health-checks and restarts the sidecar if needed.
- **Tests:** ported supervisor tests (spawn, health timeout, crash restart once, backoff, CPU fallback, stale PID, env scrub, parent death); transport tests for socket and loopback-with-key; memory-policy and load-order unit tests (cold ASR never overlaps an LLM load); abort on cancel; provider list contains `local` and no cloud entries.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** RS-06 on the real slice: signed binary under Gatekeeper on macOS 13 and 26; RSS and latency per tier incl. the cold-cold row; Esc abort ≤ 100 ms; kill the sidecar mid-take → take fails open, sidecar restarts; sleep mid-take → recovered on wake.
- **Exit criteria:** RS-06 thresholds in the real build; no network access by the sidecar (verified with nettop and the `wb-egress` run; `--offline` passed when the tag supports it).
- **Effort / depends on:** L / F-02, F-08, RS-06.
- **As built (2026-10-30), decisions that refine this block** (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Local LLM runtime (S-10)"; numbers in `docs/fork/spikes/RS-06-llm-runtime.md`):
  - Files: `fork/llm/{supervisor,process,transport,health,memory,catalog,download}.rs` as listed, plus `chat.rs` (the request), `prompt.rs` (OpenWhispr seed for Try it; S-12a grows it), `runtime.rs` (app wiring), `rs06.rs` (bench, test-only), `fork/commands/llm.rs` (`fork_llm_status`, `fork_llm_try`, `fork_llm_retry`; event `fork-llm-status`); `src/fork/settings/advanced/AiEngine.tsx` on the debug panel; `scripts/wb-translate-llama.ts` (the `llama-server` translation backend).
  - Model: Muesli 2B v12 Q4_K_M on every Apple Silicon Mac ≥ 8 GB (DEC-03), from `Muesli-HQ/qwen35-2b-muesli-cleanup-v12-gguf` at `e35af097…` (no own HF org), sha256-checked with a size+mtime marker; Qwen3.5-2B (`unsloth/Qwen3.5-2B-GGUF` at `f6d5376b…`) pinned as the fallback. The download starts at launch or after Handy's `model-download-complete` once a speech model is on disk; HF endpoint fixed, no token, stall watchdog + backoff, resumes on wake after a failure.
  - Supervisor: ported from TypeWhisper (read at `4620bb59`); restart once, then CPU (`-ngl 0`), then backoff 30 s doubling to 10 min; port races retried without counting; a crash after ready restarts once in the background, a second within 60 s waits. Parent death: a forked kqueue `NOTE_EXIT` watchdog (raw syscalls only) instead of TypeWhisper's shell loop; stale PID killed at launch only if the process is named `llama-server`.
  - **Streaming replies.** The client always sends `stream: true` and assembles the SSE stream: b11624 polls a non-streaming request for a hang-up only once per second (`HTTP_POLLING_SECONDS`), so Esc left the slot busy +590 ms; streamed, the slot is free within 19 ms p95 (RS-06).
  - **First-launch Metal compile.** The first start of a newly installed or updated sidecar takes about 3.5 s more on the M5 Max (Metal compiles the embedded shaders and caches them per binary). The runtime pays it once in the background right after the model is ready (marker `llm/warm-<size>-<mtime>`), then stops the server.
  - Ordered loads and prewarm use Handy's events (no `actions.rs` hook); the memory rule uses free + inactive + purgeable pages against the model's RSS (2,100 MiB) + 1.5 GB. On an 8 GB Mac with a speech model loaded this may often say "no": RS-06 on M1-8 must check how often the rule skips cleanup.
  - Not done here: signing with the Developer ID and notarization (F-02), macOS 13, the M1 8 GB rows and the cold-cold row (RS-06 open items), the wb-egress run, real lid-close sleep (simulated with SIGSTOP/SIGCONT and a kill while stopped).
- **Mac wave 3 fixes (2026-11-03, `fix/wave3`):** finding 3 (first take after a fresh start pasted 19 s after the stop): the launch warm-up stopped the server the take's key-down prewarm had just been handed, and the take's memory check (`is_running`) then waited under the state lock behind the restart. Now `Supervisor::warm_up` keeps its server when anything asked for it meanwhile (demand counter, callers waiting, requests in flight, a recording take via `take_started`/`take_ended` from `recording-state-event`); `is_running` never waits (a start in progress counts); every start runs as its own task, so a take that gives up at `START_BUDGET` (2.5 s; S-21's 2.8 s) pastes the raw text and the start finishes for the next take, with no half-started process; `idle_tick` never stops a server a take or a caller is holding. Note 5 (syspolicyd held a copied sidecar 58 s): decision, the 60 s first-start limit stays; a CPU fallback caused only by `/health` timeouts tries the GPU again once per launch, every launch starts on the GPU (nothing persists the fallback), and a warm-up that ended on the CPU writes no warm marker so the next launch warms Metal. Tests: 10 new supervisor state-machine tests (`supervisor/tests.rs`). **Queued:** MAC-TEST-SCRIPT S-10.9 (`scripts/wb-s10/first-take.sh`).

### S-11 Text pipeline v2

- **Goal:** the newline-safe, ordered pipeline of §4.10 with from→to rules and voice commands.
- **User-visible outcome:** "new line" and "new paragraph" work; multi-line text survives; "scratch that" deletes the previous clause; from→to dictionary rules apply (managed in S-16).
- **R2 rows closed:** D-210, D-48, D-56
- **Regression protected (HAVE):** C-67, D-15, D-17
- **R4 parts:** aho-corasick 1.1 (Unlicense/MIT, adopt direct); unicode-segmentation (adopt); VoiceTypr `src-tauri/src/writing/library_rules.rs` + 17 tests (AGPL-3.0, port; file marked AGPL) for the rule stage with protected URLs/e-mails and provenance; MacParakeet `SpokenPunctuation.swift` tables + literal escape (GPL-3.0, port data); SpeakoFlow `audio_toolkit/replacements.rs`, hyprwhspr-rs `normalize.rs` (MIT, reference); MacParakeet `spec/07-text-processing.md` (GPL, reference).
- **Handy files touched:** `managers/transcription.rs:1600-1662` (one `fork::text::process` call); ⚠ `actions.rs` (post-LLM stages run in `deliver`). `audio_toolkit/text.rs` not edited (wrapped per line).
- **New fork files:** `fork/text/{mod,lines,rules,commands,scratch,whitespace,placeholders,counts}.rs`, `fork/text/tables/commands.<lang>.json`.
- **Tests:** golden tests per stage; property test "no stage removes `\n`"; Wispr dictionary spec as acceptance (whole word, case-insensitive, literal, longest wins, ≤ 60 chars); clause-edge rule ("the period of time" stays text); Handy's existing `text.rs` tests unchanged.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** dictate a three-paragraph e-mail into Mail and a two-line Slack message (Shift+Return path in typing mode).
- **Exit criteria:** stage order and change counts recorded per take (`fix_count_dictionary`).
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-09.
- **As built (2026-10-30), decisions that change the plan above:**
  - **Hooks:** `managers/transcription.rs` 16 lines (one `fork::text::process` call wrapping Handy's call in a closure; replaces S-09's 6); no new line in `actions.rs` (the post-LLM stages are the first line of `fork::insert::deliver`). `audio_toolkit/text.rs` and Handy's `text.rs` tests untouched.
  - **Files:** as planned, plus `fork/text/fold.rs` (Unicode case folding with a byte map, so `aho-corasick` matches case-insensitively beyond ASCII and cuts the original text correctly), `fork/db/rules.rs` (active rules, `corrections` rows, hit counts), migration `0003_text.sql` (`entry_meta.text_stages`, JSON). Tables for de, en, es, fr, it, ja, pl, pt, zh. `rules.rs` is the AGPL port (NOTICE §3/§4, PORTS.md); the tables carry MacParakeet's spoken-punctuation data.
  - **Stage order:** see the §4.10 as-built note (stage 3 before Handy's script conversion; outputs protected). If a placeholder does not survive Handy's stages intact (a custom-word n-gram that ends on it glues it to the next word), that line is redone with the outputs inserted as plain text.
  - **Rules:** read from `fork.db` once per take (indexed query; recompiled only when changed), so S-16 needs no reload signal. Whole word (no boundary needed next to scripts without spaces), Unicode case-insensitive, literal output, leftmost then longest, single pass, "from" ≤ 60 characters, URLs and e-mail addresses never rewritten, older rule wins a duplicate. No per-rule language.
  - **Voice commands:** line, paragraph and "scratch that" need a clause edge on **both** sides (start/end, line break, clause punctuation, another command): the recognizer writes punctuation where the speaker pauses, so "Hello. New line. How are you?" fires and "a new line of shoes" or "Hello newline how are you" (no pauses) stays text. Spoken "?"/"!" need only the right edge and no blocker word before them (MacParakeet's set; "period" and "comma" are not commands, so "the period of time" can never change). The next word after a break is capitalized. Literal prefix ("literally", "literal", "wörtlich", …) keeps the phrase. Language: Handy's resolved output language picks its table; unknown uses all tables; a language without a table gets none. Voice commands apply to every transcription that goes through `finish_text`, file transcriptions included (Handy gives the hook no way to tell them apart).
  - **"Scratch that":** deletes back to the previous clause punctuation or line break (the sentence, when the command follows a sentence end), within the take; tidies the punctuation left behind.
  - **Per take:** `entry_meta.text_stages` = `[{"stage":"rules","changes":n},{"stage":"handy",…},{"stage":"commands",…},{"stage":"scratch",…},{"stage":"whitespace",…}]`; `fix_count_dictionary` = rule hits, also into `daily_stats.fixes_dictionary`; one `corrections` row per hit (source `rule`, replaced on retry); `dictionary_entries.hit_count`/`last_hit_at`. One log line per take without text; debug builds log the texts with `WB_DEBUG_TEXT_TRACE=1`.
  - **Latency:** the fork stages cost 2.0 ms per 200-word take with 100 rules in a debug build (unit test bound 50 ms).
  - **Real-Mac result (2026-10-31, M5 Max, macOS 26.6.2; `docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-S-11.md`):** isolated portable debug build (`com.wisprbro.s11check`), Parakeet TDT 0.6B v3, macOS TTS through the speakers into the mic, takes through SIGUSR2 → record → ASR → pipeline → delivery. Three-paragraph e-mail: 9/9 transcriptions right (`Hi Anna-Lena.\n\nThanks for the notes on Wispr Bro. I will send it on Tuesday.\n\nBest.\nFinn.`: two rules, three line commands, one scratch), 8/8 delivered `confirmed` into TextEdit and a Safari WebKit editor. Two-line chat: 4/4 TextEdit, 3/4 Chrome 156 via typing with Shift+Return and nothing sent. Literal/clause-edge 3/3, spoken "?" 2/3. The two failures were the microphone picking up other speech in the room (the pipeline left the then edge-less "New line nine eleven" as text, as specified) and one empty take. 23/23 rows with `text_stages`; `fix_count_dictionary` total 18 = 18 `corrections` rows = `daily_stats.fixes_dictionary`. Mail itself was not used (a compose window would autosave a draft into the owner's mail account); its stand-ins are TextEdit (native text view) and a WebKit contenteditable page in Safari. Slack is not installed; its stand-in is a local chat page in Chrome 156 (Enter sends, Shift+Enter is a new line), which is S-04's typing path.
  - **Not verified:** a person dictating (TTS only), Mail and Slack themselves, other languages' tables on real speech (unit tests only), the stage-9 restore on a real LLM rewrite (S-12a wires it; unit-tested).

### S-12a Cleanup levels on the local LLM

- **Goal:** Wispr's None / Light / Medium on every dictation, private and safe.
- **User-visible outcome:** every dictation is cleaned at Light by default (fillers, grammar, punctuation, self-corrections like "coffee at 2, actually 3" → "coffee at 3"). AI-chat apps (`is_ai_chat`) get no LLM formatting. If cleanup is slow, wrong or the model is not ready, the deterministic text is inserted and the pill says "Inserted without cleanup". The level picker component is built here and mounted on the fork debug panel; S-19a mounts the same component in Style › Cleanup. The second "post-process" hotkey and the Experimental gate are gone.
- **R2 rows closed:** A-209, D-01, D-02, D-03, D-04, D-05, D-09, D-11, D-12, D-13, D-16, D-208, D-49
- **R4 parts:** OpenWhispr `src/locales/*/prompts.json` `cleanupPrompt` (MIT, port seed); FreeFlow `PostProcessingService.swift:117` and `TranscriptTextCore.swift:128-182` (MIT, port); SpeakoFlow `actions.rs:1283-1460` validators (MIT, port); BetterWispr `TranscriptPolisher.swift:218-233` `accepted()` + `PolishContextTests` (GPL-3.0 VoiceInk-derived, port with VoiceInk attribution); TypeWhisper boundary wrapper and language hint (GPL, port); FluidVoice JSON-field framing (GPL, port); open-flow `needsCleanup` gate (MIT, port); Muesli `Qwen3PostProcessor.swift:110-140` assistant markers (MIT, port); similar 3.2.1 (Apache-2.0, adopt) for the LLM fix count.
- **Handy files touched:** ⚠ `actions.rs` (`post_process_transcription` → `fork::llm::post_process`, which also owns the Apple call site from `actions.rs:256-272`; Handy's bridge files untouched).
- **New fork files:** `fork/llm/{prompt,guards,post_process,deadline}.rs`, `fork/llm/prompts/*.txt` (our own text, seeded from the MIT sources), `src/fork/style/CleanupLevelPicker.tsx`.
- **Tests:** prompt builder snapshots; each guard with positive and negative cases; deadline and fail-open; AI-chat skip by `is_ai_chat`; language-kept check; dictionary terms injected and re-applied; nightly eval gate against the F-10 set with the RS-07 thresholds computed per §11.1.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** RS-07 thresholds on the real build; §5.1 Light latency on M1 8 GB and 16 GB; cleanup works offline.
- **Exit criteria:** eval thresholds met; `cleanup_outcome` recorded per take; no unparsed output ever inserted.
- **Effort / depends on:** L / S-10, S-11, RS-07.
- **As built (2026-10-31), decisions that refine this block** (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Cleanup levels (S-12a)"; evidence in `docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-S-12a.md`):
  - **Files:** `fork/llm/{post_process,guards,prompt,deadline}.rs` as planned, plus `markers.rs` + `tables/markers.json` (per-language hesitations, the owner's filler table verbatim, repair markers, stopwords, assistant openers; Muesli's markers), `leadins.rs` (sentence lead-ins removed without the model) and `eval_run.rs` (the eval through the shipped path). `fork/llm/prompts/{cleanup,medium,vocabulary,language}.txt`: `cleanup.txt` is OpenWhispr's `cleanupPrompt` byte for byte (MIT; the RS-07 winner, so Light without extras is exactly what RS-07 measured); the rest is our own text. `src/fork/style/CleanupLevelPicker.tsx` on the debug panel (with the last take's result). Command `fork_llm_last_cleanup`, event `ForkCleanupResult` (`notice` = "Inserted without cleanup"; S-03's bar shows `PillMessage::InsertedWithoutCleanup` and "Cleaning up…" from `fork::pill::cleaning_up`).
  - **Hooks:** `actions.rs` 2 lines (`process_transcription_output` → `crate::fork::llm::post_process`, same signature and result; Handy's chain, Apple call included, stays and runs only where the fork does not own cleanup: headless CLI, a plain Handy build). Added beyond the plan: `managers/transcription_jobs.rs` (3 lines: retries and file transcriptions are cleaned too, without the AI-chat skip) and `managers/transcription.rs` (2 lines: Handy's Info line "Transcription result: …" now logs `[N chars]` through `fork::diagnostics::redact`; the text only in debug builds with `WB_DEBUG_TEXT_TRACE`). Budgets in `ci/hooks.txt`.
  - **Order per take:** plan (level; AI-chat skip by the frontmost app's `is_ai_chat`, dictations only; model not ready; memory rule) → lead-ins of the take's language (deterministic: "nou dus …", "alors du coup …", "so …", "also …"; Muesli leaves Dutch and French lead-ins in place) → Light gate (open-flow's `needsCleanup` extended: no filler/repair/stutter, capitalized and punctuated → no request) → blocks of ≤ 120 words (a short multi-line text is one request; DEC-51 paragraph by paragraph for long takes) → guard chain → a reply that only joined the lines is **realigned** to the input's line breaks and guarded again; one that made a list is resent line by line → fail-open per block (the deterministic text, with sentence-opening hesitations also removed) → stage 9 (`note_rewrite` + `finish`) → record.
  - **Language:** the take's language from Handy's pipeline (now kept in the S-11 `TextRecord`), else a **hint** guessed from the text among the user's `spoken_languages` (word votes over the tables, whatlang ≥ 0.8 from 8 words). The hint drives the prompt's language fragment (non-English only; it names that language's filler words) and the lead-in stage; the guards use only a known language (else they detect the input's), so a wrong guess cannot reject a correct cleanup. English gets no fragment, keeping RS-07's prompt.
  - **Guards** (R5 order; all run, every reason reported): `json`, SpeakoFlow `empty`/`punctuation_only`/`channel`/`prompt_echo` (+ the user-turn framing, RS-07 `clean-008`)/`too_long`, FreeFlow `executed_instruction`, BetterWispr `word_band` (lower bound on the words that must stay) / `question_mark` / `new_tokens` (a joined word or an added/dropped ending is not new), `assistant_marker` (Muesli list + openers), `entity` (digits, URLs, e-mails, code spans, dictionary words; a number inside a self-repair may go; **no new digits**: no ITN at Light), `line_count`, `formatting` (ours: no bullets, numbering, headings or bold the input lacked), `content_words` (ours, RS-07 rec. 5: stopwords, the filler table and up to 4 words before a repair marker excluded; Light lets 1 in 10 go, Medium 1 in 4; a look-alike substitution, "negeer" → "Neger", "thanks" → "tanks", never), `language` (Handy `lang_id`).
  - **Deadlines:** request = R5 formula; take = the same formula with its 6 s cap per started 200 words (24 s at most), plus 2.5 s for a cold server start (§5.1 cold row); a late block keeps its deterministic text. Debug builds: `WB_DEBUG_CLEANUP_DEADLINE_MS` forces the deadline (Mac test A6.1).
  - **Dictionary terms:** the prompt's vocabulary is the dictionary's words (Handy's custom words, S-16's mirror) and the replacements' outputs; the guards require back only the words; the replacements' outputs come back through stage 9 instead of failing the cleanup.
  - **Record:** `entry_meta.cleanup_level`, `cleanup_engine` (`local`; `none` when no request was made), `cleanup_outcome` (`applied`/`skipped`/`guard`/`timeout`/`error`; not-ready, low-memory, AI-chat, clean-text and level-None takes are `skipped` with their reason in the event), `fix_count_llm` = runs of changed words (the S-11 LCS extended; **similar 3.2.1 not adopted**: the in-tree LCS does the job without a new crate), `daily_stats.fixes_llm`. No `corrections` rows for LLM edits (S-25 decides). History's `post_processed_text` is the cleaned text.
  - **Level None** also turns Handy's filler and stutter removal off (R5 §4.10 stage 5): new `ENFORCED` rule `filler_word_removal_enabled`, mirrored at once on change. "Try it" now cleans exactly like a dictation (level, gate, guards) and says why when it would not apply.
  - **Not adopted:** FluidVoice's JSON-field framing and TypeWhisper's boundary wrapper (Muesli was trained on and measured with OpenWhispr's `<transcript>` framing; dictated tags are stripped, BetterWispr `delimited`). Apple FM is S-12b.
  - **Eval (S-12a.1, M5 Max):** 271 items (42 new nl/fr items labelled under the owner's policy, `labels.revised = "s12a-nl-fr-2026-10-31"`; Dutch/French stopword lists extended, content words regenerated; items sha256 `006789471e93…`), shipped path on the real sidecar, `--replay` with Handy's `lang_id`. **Light: damage 0.0 % (0/271), answer 0.0 % (0/117), filler 95.0 % (114/120), language 100 % (59/59; strict 58/59), guard catch 100 % (7/7): all RS-07 gates pass.** Per language: en filler 95.6 %, nl 94.4 %, fr 95.5 %, damage 0 % each. Medium: damage 0.0 %, catch 100 %. Without any language (no spoken languages set) filler drops to 81.7 % (nl 66.7 %, fr 68.2 %). LLM time per item p50 115 ms, p95 183 ms.
  - **Real app (M5 Max, TTS):** 42 Light takes in en/nl/fr + a forced timeout + level None into TextEdit; key-up → text p50 230 ms / p95 532 ms on the final build (all builds p50 290 / p95 870 ms), far inside §5.1; one damaging take on an intermediate build (repair reach 8 words) led to the 4-word reach.
  - **Not verified:** M1 8 GB / 16 GB latency, the bar's messages (product builds only), level None's Handy mirror in a test-identifier build (unit-tested), a real stage-9 restore, a real AI-chat app, a person's voice. The second labelling pass of the eval set is still open (F-10).

### S-12b Apple Foundation Models path

- **Goal:** use Apple's on-device model where it is faster, without touching Handy's bridge.
- **User-visible outcome:** on macOS 26 with Apple Intelligence on, cleanup can run on Apple's model (default or fallback per RS-08), with the same guards and fail-open behaviour; elsewhere nothing changes.
- **R2 rows closed:** D-68
- **R4 parts:** hex `apple_intelligence.swift`/`.rs` (MIT, port) + MacParakeet `FoundationModelsAppleIntelligence*` error mapping and TypeWhisper prewarm call sites (GPL, port), all into fork files.
- **Handy files touched:** none (the call site already goes through `fork::llm::post_process` from S-12a; `build.rs`, `apple_intelligence.rs` and `swift/apple_intelligence.swift` untouched).
- **New fork files:** `src-tauri/crates/wb-apple-fm/{Cargo.toml,build.rs,src/lib.rs,swift/fork_apple_fm.swift}` (own Swift build, unique symbol prefix `wb_apple_fm_`), `fork/llm/apple.rs` (availability reasons, prewarm, `spawn_blocking` with a cancelling timeout).
- **Tests:** availability reasons mapping; cancel and timeout; guard chain applied to Apple output; build on a machine without the macOS 26 SDK uses the stub.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** RS-08 thresholds on the real build.
- **Exit criteria:** RS-08 decision applied (default or fallback-only); no symbol clash with Handy's bridge.
- **Effort / depends on:** S / S-12a, RS-08.
- **As built (2026-10-31), decisions that refine this block** (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Apple Foundation Models (S-12b)"; spike `docs/fork/spikes/RS-08-apple-fm.md`; evidence `docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-S-12b.md`):
  - **RS-08 decision: fallback only** (`fork::llm::apple::ROLE = Fallback`). Apple's model cleans a take only when the sidecar cannot: model not ready (downloading, failed, runtime missing) **or the R5 memory rule says no** (added: Apple's model runs in a system process outside our 2.1 GB budget, so on a tight Mac it beats no cleanup). An AI-chat target still gets no LLM. A take in a known language Apple's model does not list (`supportedLanguages`, e.g. Polish) is not sent; an unknown language is, and an `unsupportedLanguageOrLocale` reply fails open.
  - **Files:** as planned: `crates/wb-apple-fm/{Cargo.toml,build.rs,src/lib.rs,swift/fork_apple_fm.swift}` and `fork/llm/apple.rs`; plus `fork/llm/rs08.rs` (latency bench, test-only), `src/fork/settings/advanced/AppleEngine.tsx` (the Apple Intelligence row of the AI engine panel, debug panel until S-14), command `fork_llm_apple_status`, `ForkLlmTryResult.engine`; `scripts/wb-s12b/` (RS-08 runner, takes, clipboard save/restore).
  - **Bridge:** its own Swift build (`-module-name WbAppleFm`, static lib, weak FoundationModels), seven `@_cdecl` symbols all `wb_apple_fm_*` (unit test against Handy's names; both bridges link into one binary). Without the macOS 26 SDK, full Xcode or Apple Silicon (or with `WB_APPLE_FM_STUB=1`) it sets `cfg(wb_apple_fm_stub)` and builds no Swift; the stub reports `Unsupported` (tested with `WB_APPLE_FM_STUB=1`). hex's `blocking(timeout:)` + `task.cancel()`, availability codes 0-5 with a 10 s cache, `contextSize`; MacParakeet's `GenerationError` mapping (+ macOS 27's `LanguageModelError`); request ids so Rust cancels a request it dropped (Esc, the take bound); errors are numeric codes, never text.
  - **Request:** the same system prompt and user turn as the sidecar (OpenWhispr prompt + JSON line), `permissiveContentTransformations`, greedy sampling, the sidecar's `max_tokens`, a **plain reply** (RS-08: guided `{text}` generation was worse on every metric and slower); the reply goes through the same guard chain as `{"text": …}`. Request and take deadlines are S-12a's.
  - **Prewarm:** at key-down (`recording-state-event` `started`), only when Apple's model would clean the take, a session with the last Apple take's instructions (else the level's plain prompt) is prewarmed; the next request with the same instructions uses it once (TypeWhisper only keeps it alive).
  - **Record:** `cleanup_engine = apple` (the enum value existed since S-12a) in `entry_meta` and `ForkCleanupResult`; a refusal, guardrail, `rateLimited`, cancelled or unsupported-language error is outcome `error` with the "Inserted without cleanup" notice; counters (requests, refusals, rate limited) on the panel.
  - **Debug-only:** `WB_DEBUG_LLM_HOLD_MODEL=1` keeps the local model "not ready" (never checked or downloaded) so the fallback can be tested; `WISPRBRO_SELFCHECK_APPLE=1|local` runs plan + run on our own 50-word samples with the app in the background and logs a PASS/FAIL summary.
  - **Hooks:** none in Handy files; `src-tauri/Cargo.toml` +1 line in the fork dependency block (budget +1).
  - **Real Mac (M5 Max, macOS 26.6.2, Apple Intelligence on; `docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-S-12b.md`):** RS-08 through the shipped path (above); the app as a background app with the local model held "not ready": 38/38 takes on Apple's model, 35 applied, 3 guard, **0 `rateLimited`**, 0 refusals, prewarmed p50 1227 ms; three real dictations (TTS) reached Apple's model, two were rejected by the content-word guard and went in as spoken; Try it and the Apple Intelligence row on the debug panel; with the model in place 38/38 on the local engine, 0 Apple requests.
  - **Not verified:** Apple Intelligence off or an ineligible Mac (unit and UI tests only; the setting was not changed), the memory-rule fallback on M1 8 GB, macOS 26.0-26.3 and 27 at runtime, a person's voice.

### Hub and features

### S-13 Hub shell

- **Goal:** Wispr's calm Hub chrome as the home for every feature.
- **User-visible outcome:** a warm light (and dark) window with a left sidebar: Dictation, Insights, Dictionary, Snippets, Style, Transforms, Scratchpad; at the bottom Share with a friend, Settings, Help; a slot for the setup checklist card (filled in S-30). Collapse button, header with theme toggle and notifications bell, serif display headings, rounded cards, example banners and empty states with one clear action, "New!"/"Beta" tags. The footer shows background-job status; S-28 adds the version and update status to the same footer.
- **R2 rows closed:** C-01, C-14, C-15, C-16, C-19, C-21, C-22, C-23, C-24, C-25, C-26, C-204, C-205, C-206, C-210
- **Regression protected (HAVE):** C-20, C-218
- **R4 parts:** Radix Primitives (dropdown-menu 2.2.1, popover 1.3.1, tabs 1.2.1, context-menu, collapsible; MIT, adopt); sonner 2.0.x and lucide-react (in tree); Newsreader 5.3.0 + Source Serif 4 5.3.1 (OFL-1.1, vendor woff2 subsets via the existing subset pipeline); Handy color tokens and `Dialog`/`Tooltip` (keep).
- **Handy files touched:** ⚠ `src/App.tsx` (render `ForkApp`); `Sidebar.tsx` untouched; main window size/min size set in `fork::on_ready` (no `lib.rs` edit).
- **New fork files:** `src/fork/hub/{ForkApp,Sidebar,Header,Footer,nav,dropRouter}.tsx`, `src/fork/ui/{Banner,EmptyState,Tag,Menu,Tabs}.tsx`, `src/fork/styles/{tokens.css,fonts.css}`, `src/assets/fonts/fork/*`.
- **Tests:** Playwright: every nav item, keyboard-only traversal, `aria-current`, banner dismissal persists, theme switch, deep link `navigate(page, section)`, file drop routing by page (a test page registers a drop zone; a drop outside any zone is ignored and never transcribed, RE-33; the Dictionary import case is tested in S-16), axe checks.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** overlay title bar drags the window (C-25 drag permission check); VoiceOver reads the sidebar.
- **Exit criteria:** every page reachable by keyboard; light/dark contrast AA.
- **Effort / depends on:** L / F-05, F-07.
- **As built (2026-10-30):** decisions that refine this block (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Hub (S-13)"; real-Mac and Playwright evidence in the S-13 report):
  - **Settings until S-14** is a page slot that hosts Handy's own sections unchanged as tabs (`src/fork/hub/HandySettingsPage.tsx`; section = tab, so `navigate("settings", "history")` deep-links), keeping every Handy setting reachable. S-14 replaces it with the modal. History is a drop zone there, so Handy's "drop audio to transcribe" survives, scoped to that page (RE-33). Placeholder pages for slices not built yet show an empty state with one action (a deep link where an interim home exists: Dictionary → Settings › Advanced custom words, Dictation → History).
  - **Help** is a Radix menu (non-modal) with an Essentials group of deep links (shortcut and microphone, models and languages, history); S-26b adds Updates and Get in touch. **Notifications** bell opens a Radix popover with an empty state; S-18 fills it through `setNotificationsPanel`. Checklist card slot: `setSidebarCard` (S-30).
  - **Theme toggle** in the header flips light ⇄ dark in one click and saves Handy's `theme`; System stays in Settings › About (S-14 General later). Tags: Transforms and Scratchpad "Beta", Insights "New!" (cleared on first visit through `seen_new_tags`). Sidebar collapse state is a per-window UI convenience in `localStorage` (no fork setting).
  - **Window:** default page area 1040 × 720 (shrunk to the screen's work area less 48 pt), minimum Handy's 720 × 600 page; frame adds the measured title bar (32 pt on macOS 26). Set in `fork/hub.rs` (a new top-level fork module, added to §4.1). The overlay title bar is set at Ready for onboarded users and by `ForkApp` on mount, restored on unmount (onboarding screens keep the standard bar); title text hidden. Capabilities: `core:window:allow-start-dragging` and `core:window:allow-set-title-bar-style` in `capabilities/fork.json` (`core:default` grants neither, C-25).
  - **Footer:** background jobs (Handy's `TranscriptionStatus`) plus Handy's update checker and version until S-28 replaces them; Handy's model selector is gone (B-207).
  - **Fonts:** Newsreader (Latin, Vietnamese) and Source Serif 4 (Cyrillic, Greek) are Fontsource's own per-script woff2 subsets, copied and sha256-pinned by `scripts/wb-fonts.ts` (no re-subsetting needed); `src/assets/fonts/fork/**` added to the guards' fork paths.
  - **Tokens:** `src/fork/styles/tokens.css` also retunes Handy's background, text, `--color-ink-2` and `--color-ink-3` (Handy's greys measured 3.9-4.2:1, below AA).
  - **Debug host** stays its own root (F-05 planned to move it into `ForkApp`): it must open over onboarding, and the self-check needs it in any state.
  - **package.json:** npm/bun have no comment keys inside dependency maps, so the §4.2 "`//fork` comment key" cannot mark fork dependencies; they are listed in `ci/hooks.txt` and ARCHITECTURE.md instead. `src/App.tsx` hook budget: 42 changed lines (import + JSX swap; Handy's unused helpers left in place for clean merges).
  - Axe runs on every Hub page and popup in light and dark, but excludes Handy's hosted settings sections (`[data-handy-content]`), which have serious findings of their own (unnamed icon buttons, a disallowed ARIA attribute); S-14 rebuilds them and S-37 audits them.

The panels first mounted on the fork debug panel move here unchanged. The hotkeys panel lists every action with edit, delete, add (≤ 4), Reset to default, Done; it opens from Settings, Help › Shortcuts and onboarding.
- **R2 rows closed:** A-33, A-65, B-55, B-56, B-67, C-12, C-79, C-80, C-84, C-98
- **Regression protected (HAVE):** A-36, A-64, B-61, B-66, C-88, C-92, D-214
- **R4 parts:** Radix (adopt); Handy setting components (reuse by import); Tauri activation-policy code (keep).
- **Handy files touched:** ⚠ `lib.rs:129-163` (show-in-dock hook).
- **New fork files:** `src/fork/settings/{SettingsModal,General,System,DataPrivacy,Advanced,About}.tsx`, `src/fork/settings/hotkeys/{HotkeysPanel,BindingRow,Capture}.tsx`.
- **Tests:** Playwright for each section, deep links into sections, hotkey capture (refused under Secure Input with an explanation), conflicts, reset; Dock toggle survives window close; Advanced choices persist across relaunch (F-07 lists).
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** Dock toggle on macOS 26 (activation-policy flips); launch-at-login after reboot.
- **Exit criteria:** no technical control outside Advanced; every existing Handy setting still reachable.

- **As built (2026-11-01):** `src/fork/settings/{SettingsModal,General,System,DataPrivacy,Advanced,About,Controls}.tsx` provides the two-column native dialog; the existing Handy controls and the fork microphone, sounds, models, AI-engine, cleanup and bar controls moved into their product sections, with technical choices under collapsed Advanced. `settings/hotkeys/` is the single panel for built-in and runtime actions, with add/edit/delete (four per action), activation choices, conflicts, Secure Input explanation, Reset and Done, reachable from Settings, Help › Shortcuts and onboarding. Scratchpad open behaviour is a typed fork setting for S-24. The permitted `lib.rs` close guard and fork activation-policy update preserve Dock behaviour; History keeps the scoped audio drop zone. Generated bindings were regenerated through the binding test. Automated gates: 1,330 Rust tests passed, 293 Playwright passed/10 skipped (48 focused S-14 specs, including keyboard, RTL, reduced motion and axe), guards, ESLint, translation and format checks passed. All 25 locale batches landed machine-flagged; one `zh-TW` Scratchpad label remains English and explicitly `untranslated`. **Not run:** macOS 26 Dock activation-policy/window-close behaviour and launch-at-login through a reboot; these are queued for the consolidated UI Mac wave. **Mac wave 1 (2026-11-02):** Dock toggle PASS (activation policy flips both ways, close keeps it); launch at login through a reboot still needs the owner (`docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-macwave-1.md`).

### S-15a Dictation home and stats rail

- **Goal:** Wispr's Dictation home: greeting, stats rail and a promo banner.
- **User-visible outcome:** "Hello, <first name>" (from macOS, overridable); a right rail with total words, words per minute and day streak, plus a Voice Profile progress bar (unlocks at 1,700 words, DEC-50); one dismissible promo banner; the rail refreshes live after each take.
