- **New fork files:** `src/fork/home/{Home,Greeting,StatsRail,VoiceProfileBar,PromoBanner}.tsx`, `fork/commands/stats.rs`.
- **Tests:** Playwright: greeting fallback when no name; stats live refresh (`fork-stats-updated` event); banner dismissal; numbers match a fixture.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** 10 takes update the rail live.
- **Exit criteria:** home is the landing page after onboarding; the rail matches `daily_stats`.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-09, S-13.
- **As built (2026-10-30):** decisions that refine this block (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Dictation home (S-15a)"; real-app evidence in `docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-S-15a.md`):
  - **Files:** as planned plus `src/fork/home/useStatsSummary.ts` (the live read). `fork/commands/stats.rs` holds only `fork_account_name` (first word of `NSFullUserName`, surrounding punctuation trimmed, ≤ 64 chars, `None` off macOS); `fork_stats_summary` stays in S-09's `commands/history.rs`. No Handy file touched, no new event (S-09's `ForkStatsUpdated` drives the refresh).
  - **Greeting:** "Hello, <name>" is the page's `h1` (our wording; without a name just "Hello"). Name order: the `greeting_name` fork setting (F-07 already had it) → macOS first name → none. The override is set in place: a pencil button opens a Radix popover (name field, Save, "Use my Mac account name" or, without one, "Remove the name"); saving the Mac's own name stores no override. S-14 may also list it under General. `Popover` gained an optional controlled mode.
  - **Rail:** total words, words per minute (rounded; a dash read as "Not enough speech yet" under S-09's 3 s threshold) and the current streak, as a `<dl>` in a labelled `aside`, numbers grouped per the app language. It shows `fork_stats_summary` unchanged, so it matches `daily_stats` by construction. Refresh: each `ForkStatsUpdated`, window focus/visibility and just after local midnight (streak roll-over); overlapping triggers collapse into one extra read; `NotReady` is retried at 1-16 s. No polling.
  - **Voice profile:** `role="progressbar"` (value text "N of 1,700 words") over Handy's `ProgressLine`, recoloured; at 1,700 words it says unlocked and links to Insights (`navigate("insights", "voice")`, filled by S-25).
  - **Banner:** S-13's dictation banner (id `hub.dictation.start`, so an earlier dismissal holds), now `PromoBanner.tsx`; the "Show me how" action arrives with S-32's tutorial.
  - **Layout:** a container query on the page width: ≥ 680 px greeting on top, main column + 240 px rail; narrower (the 720 × 600 minimum window with the sidebar open) one column with the rail first. History (S-15b) gets the main column; until then an empty state links to Settings › History, worded by whether there are dictations.
  - **Translations:** 24 new `home.*` keys machine-translated with the `llama-server` backend (Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507), the worst literal results corrected by hand; all flagged `machine` for S-37.
  - **Real app (M5 Max, portable profile `com.wisprbro.s15acheck`):** 30 seeded rows backfilled by S-09's reconcile → rail 355 words / 142 wpm / streak 5 = `daily_stats`; 10 `say` takes through SIGUSR2 updated the rail live after each take (459 / 138 / 5), same after a relaunch in dark. Not run: VoiceOver by a person, fn-held takes by a person.

### S-15b History and row actions

- **Goal:** a history the user can act on.
- **User-visible outcome:** history grouped by date with search; each row shows the final text and on hover: play, copy, flag, and ⋮ (Undo AI edit / Redo AI edit, Retry transcript, Delete transcript, Save audio as…). Delete shows "Deleted · Undo". Background jobs and file transcriptions appear here (files tagged, not counted).
- **R2 rows closed:** C-34, C-35, C-36, C-37, C-38, C-39, C-40, C-41, C-42, C-44, C-45, C-207
- **Regression protected (HAVE):** B-211, C-43, C-47, C-48, C-211
- **R4 parts:** @tanstack/react-virtual 3.14 (MIT, adopt); Radix dropdown (adopt); sonner Undo toast (in tree); tauri-plugin-dialog (in tree) for Save audio.
- **Handy files touched:** none (reads through fork commands; Handy history commands reused for audio and retry).
- **New fork files:** `src/fork/history/{HistoryList,HistoryRow,RowMenu,FlagDialog,Search}.tsx`, `fork/commands/history.rs`.
- **Rules:** copy uses the shown variant (C-39, RE-04); Undo AI edit toggles `shown_variant` and never re-pastes (DEC-19); Flag opens "Wrong word?" → pick a word, type the correction → adds a word or rule through the S-16 store (DEC-18); Retry keeps `previous_text` and offers a different cleanup level; Save audio is disabled with "Audio removed after 14 days" when expired.
- **Tests:** Playwright for every row action, keyboard focus-within, search, grouping across midnight and time zones; flag writes a dictionary entry; 10,000-row scroll performance.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** play audio (CSP check), retry, undo AI edit, delete/undo, flag → word appears in Dictionary.
- **Exit criteria:** all R1 §4 history behaviours present.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-09, S-12a, S-13, S-15a, S-16.
- **As built (2026-10-31), decisions that refine this block** (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "History and row actions (S-15b)"; real-app evidence in `docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-S-15b.md`):
  - **Files:** `src/fork/history/{HistoryList,HistoryRow,RowMenu,FlagDialog,Search}.tsx` as planned, plus `useHistory.ts` (store and live updates) and `dates.ts` (local-day groups); `fork/db/history_list.rs` (+ tests) is the read side (Handy rows through `HandyHistory`, which gained `page`, merged with `entry_meta`); `fork/commands/history.rs` gained `fork_history_{page,find,rows,set_variant,retry,flag,save_audio}`. History replaced S-15a's slot in the home's main column. **No migration**: `shown_variant`, `previous_text`, `flagged` (0001) and `inserted_text` (0006) were already there.
  - **Handy files:** one hook line changed, not "none": `managers/transcription_jobs.rs` passes `&entry` instead of `entry.post_process_requested` to `fork::llm::post_process_job`, so a retry can carry its own cleanup level (budget 5 → 9: rustfmt joins the three arguments on one line). Handy's retry job, `get_audio_file_path`, `AudioPlayer`, `copyToClipboard` and the file picker are reused unchanged. `package.json` +1 (`@tanstack/react-virtual` 3.14.23, the R4 pin).
  - **Undo AI edit:** "raw" is Handy's `transcription_text`, the deterministic text before the LLM (S-11 rules, Handy's filler removal and word fix applied), not `asr_text`: that is what the AI edited. Offered only when `post_processed_text` exists and differs. It changes what History shows and copies (`shown_variant`), never the stats, the search index or the target app (DEC-19). Redo AI edit flips it back.
  - **Copy (C-39):** the shown variant; for the final one `inserted_text` when a snippet expanded (S-17), else `final_text`. The row shows `final_text` (the spoken words, what search and stats use), so a snippet row shows the trigger and copies the saved text.
  - **Retry (C-43):** ⋮ offers "Retry transcript" (the cleanup setting) and the two other levels ("without cleanup", "with Light/Medium cleanup"); the level rides a per-entry slot the job takes once (`post_process::set_retry_level`). The replaced text goes to `previous_text` (S-09); a retry also resets `shown_variant` to `final` and clears `inserted_text`. "Without cleanup" skips the LLM; Handy's filler removal still follows the global level. A restore-previous-text action was not built (the text is kept for one).
  - **Flag (DEC-18):** "Wrong word?" dialog: the row's words as buttons (`Intl.Segmenter`), a pick widens to ≤ 4 neighbouring words, a correction field, "Replace it every time" (dictionary replacement, default) or "Teach the spelling" (dictionary word). Saved through S-16's store with source `flag`; conflicts offer "Replace it" (edits the existing entry); a saved or duplicate entry sets `entry_meta.flagged` and writes a `corrections` row (source `flag`, S-25 counts it). Handy's star ("saved") is not shown: text is kept forever (DEC-07).
  - **Audio:** `available` / `expired` (retention; Play and Save disabled, labelled "Audio removed after N days" from the audio retention setting) / `missing` (full disk or file gone: "No recording for this dictation"; Retry needs audio too). Play loads the file first and opens Handy's player already playing. **Portable mode:** the recordings dir is added to the asset-protocol scope at Ready (only when outside `$APPDATA`), so Play works in test profiles too. Save audio as… = the dialog plugin's save panel (`dictation-YYYY-MM-DD-HHMM.wav`) + an atomic copy in Rust.
  - **List:** grouped by the local day of `created_at` in the Mac's zone at display time (Today, Yesterday, weekday + date, year when not this year), re-labelled just after local midnight; rows in Handy's id order. Actions show on hover and `focus-within` and stay in the tab order. Search: a button that opens a field, 200 ms debounce, S-09's index paged by offset (100 per page); Esc clears and refocuses. Delete: S-09's soft delete, toast "Deleted · Undo" for 9 s (purge at 10 s). Background jobs show "Transcribing… N%" in the row (Handy's job store); file rows are tagged with the file name, practice rows "Practice" (no producer before S-29b). "Transcribe a file…" in the section header (Handy's picker).
  - **Scale (C-207):** pages of 100 from Handy's cursor (soft-deleted rows refilled from the next page), a virtual list in the Hub's scroll area; Playwright: 10,000 rows read in 100 pages on demand, < 80 rows ever in the DOM, scroll-step p95 within budget (see the run); Rust: 10,000 rows paged in debug in well under the 20 s bound.
  - **Translations:** the 80 new `history.*` keys machine-translated with the `llama-server` backend (Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507), flagged `machine`; the worst literal results (de, nl, fr) corrected by hand; S-15a's three `home.history.*` keys removed.
  - **Real app (M5 Max, release build, portable profile `com.wisprbro.s15bcheck`, seeded synthetic history with `say -o` recordings, driven through AX by `scripts/wb-s15b/ax.swift`):** day groups across midnight, file tag and the expired row's disabled Play; Play under the release CSP (player at 0:01 of 0:02, no CSP/asset error); Undo/Redo AI edit with Copy of each variant and the stats unchanged; Retry at Medium (new text, `previous_text` kept; the LLM step timed out under a load average > 100, fail-open); Delete with Undo, then the purge through Handy; Flag "Sioban → Siobhán" on the Dictionary page with source `flag`. Found: Handy's player starts a lazy source only from its own button, so Play now resolves the file first. **Not verified:** Save audio as… in the real app (the panel needs focus; another app stayed frontmost), a Medium retry actually applied, the 10,000-row scroll in WebKit (Chromium only: p95 86 ms per screen step at load ~150), VoiceOver by ear.

### S-16 Dictionary

- **Goal:** Wispr's Dictionary page on a richer local store.
- **User-visible outcome:** Dictionary page with tabs All / Added by you / Suggested (Suggested fills in S-34), search, sort, refresh, "Add word" dialog (word, or "Replace a word" from → to; the Add button stays disabled until the fields are filled), a dismissible banner "Teach Wispr Bro your words" with example chips, Import and Export. Import is a four-step wizard: (1) choose or drop a `.csv` (≤ 3 MB, with a link to our accepted-format help page), (2) map columns and preview, (3) review duplicates and conflicts, (4) done with counts. Apostrophes and non-ASCII words work. Existing Handy custom words are migrated once.
- **R2 rows closed:** C-58, C-60, C-61, C-62, C-64, C-65, C-68, D-47
- **Regression protected (HAVE):** none
- **R4 parts:** papaparse 5.7.0 (MIT, adopt); csv 1.4 (Unlicense/MIT, adopt); MacParakeet `VocabularyImportExportService` versioned format (GPL, reference).
- **Handy files touched:** none (words are mirrored into Handy's `custom_words` setting through its existing command; rules are never fed to the recognizer).
- **New fork files:** `fork/db/migrations/0003_dictionary.sql`, `fork/dictionary/{store,migrate,import,export}.rs`, `src/fork/dictionary/{DictionaryPage,AddWordDialog,ImportWizard}.tsx`, `docs/fork/user/import-format.md`.
- **Tests:** one-time migration of existing custom words (idempotent); ≤ 60 chars rule; CSV formula-injection guard (`= + - @`), 3 MB cap; dedupe; words vs rules routing; wizard steps; a `.csv` dropped on the import step is imported, never transcribed (C-205).
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** add "Siobhán" and "gonna → going to"; dictate both in Mail.
- **Exit criteria:** the dictionary drives stage 3/4 of the pipeline; Dictionary page counts update live.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-11, S-13.
- **As built (2026-10-31), decisions that change the plan above** (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Dictionary (S-16)"; real-app evidence in `docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-S-16.md`):
  - **Files:** migration `0004_dictionary.sql` (0003 was taken by S-11): `dictionary_entries.norm` (duplicate key, a Rust Unicode case fold, filled for older rows by a migration hook that also removes a newer duplicate; unique per kind) and `updated_at`, plus `dictionary_handy_mirror`. `fork/dictionary/{mod,store,migrate,import,export,tests}.rs`, `fork/commands/dictionary.rs` (9 `fork_dictionary_*` commands, event `ForkDictionaryChanged`). Frontend `src/fork/dictionary/{DictionaryPage,AddWordDialog,ImportWizard,useDictionary}`; the four-step wizard itself is shared in `src/fork/ui/{ImportWizard.tsx,importTable.ts}` for S-17. `docs/fork/user/import-format.md`. No Handy file touched.
  - **Words vs replacements:** one store; words are mirrored into Handy's `custom_words` through Handy's settings store (what its `update_custom_words` command does), never replacements. The mirror is a three-way merge against the last list written, so the one-time migration of a Handy user's words, and words still added or removed in Handy's own Settings › Advanced editor (hosted until S-14), all land in the dictionary; a word deleted on the page is never taken back. Synced at Ready, after every change and when the page reads the list.
  - **Rules:** word or "from" ≤ 60 characters, replacement ≤ 200, no line breaks or tabs; whitespace tidied; case-only replacements allowed (`jira → Jira`). Same word with the same spelling (or the same replacement) is a duplicate; same key otherwise is a conflict (dialog: "Replace it"; import: per row "use the file's"). A suggested or rejected entry (S-34) becomes active when added. At most 5,000 entries (Handy's fuzzy fix compares every word on every take).
  - **Page:** tabs All / Added by you / Suggested with live counts; search ignores case and accents; sort newest, oldest, A-Z, Z-A, most used; refresh; "Used N times" per replacement, refreshed after each take; delete with an Undo toast; banner `dictionary.teach` "Teach Wispr Bro your words" with four example chips.
  - **Import:** papaparse in the webview (delimiter detected, so `;` files work), Rust reads the file (`.csv`, ≤ 3 MB checked before and while reading; UTF-8 with or without BOM, UTF-16 with BOM, else Windows-1252), review and commit in Rust in one transaction. "Accepted format" opens in place in the wizard, not a web page (the app opens no site). Drops: step 1 is a drop zone; anywhere else a dropped file is ignored. Export: `csv` crate, UTF-8 BOM, `phrase,replacement`, formula guard `'` before `= + - @` (removed again on import), atomic write to the save-panel path.
  - **Packages:** papaparse 5.7.0 + @types/papaparse 5.5.2; `csv` 1.4, `encoding_rs` 0.8 (in tree). `ci/hooks.txt` budgets: Cargo.toml 15, package.json 39.
  - **Translations:** 158 new `fork` keys machine-translated with the `llama-server` backend (Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507), flagged `machine`; the example names kept as written in every locale and the German banner title corrected by hand.
  - **Real app (M5 Max, portable profile `com.wisprbro.s16check`, Parakeet TDT 0.6B v3, `say` through the speakers):** Handy's two custom words migrated on first launch; "gonna → going to" added through the dialog (driven by `scripts/wb-s16/ui.swift` in the test app's own window); the same TTS take went from `I'm gonna call Sioban …` to `I'm going to call Siobhan …`; the row showed "Used 1 time" live; `hit_count`, `corrections` and `fix_count_dictionary` all 1.
  - **Found:** the word "Siobhán" does not fix "Siobhan" on Parakeet: the model takes no vocabulary and Handy's fuzzy word fix is ASCII-only (D-47). A replacement `Siobhan → Siobhán` works. Candidate for S-38 (accent-folded matching for words), not done here; **done in S-38 part 2** (`dictionary_accents` stage).
  - **Not verified:** Mail itself (TextEdit stand-in), a person's voice, VoiceOver, a Finder drag onto the import step and the Export save panel by a person (MAC-TEST-SCRIPT S-16.4), a model with decoder vocabulary.
- **Owner test 1 fix (2026-11-03, `fix/owner-test-1`):** "adding a word didn't work" was the stuck `fork.db` (F-06). The save path itself was right; what the user saw was not: the dialog said only "That couldn't be saved. Please try again." and the page "isn't available yet". A failed save now says why (`Internal`: the data file can't be opened, quit and reopen, Help › Report a problem; `NotReady`: still starting) and keeps the dialog open with the text; the History flag dialog uses the same messages; a list read failing with `Internal` shows "Your dictionary can't be opened". Four new strings, translated in all 25 locales and flagged `ai-reviewed`. Playwright: `dictionary.spec.ts` "dictionary failures" (2).

### S-17 Snippets

- **Goal:** Wispr's snippets: say a trigger, get the saved text.
- **User-visible outcome:** Snippets page (list, search, sort, Add new, Import/Export, banner with three examples). Saying "my calendar link" alone or inside a sentence expands to the saved text, newlines included. Bodies are plain text (DEC-12).
- **Matching rules (Wispr spec):** alone or inline; internal punctuation must match inline; spoken case ignored; expansion keeps its saved casing; longest match wins; possessives expand ("my email's"), "n't" contractions do not; trigger ≤ 60 chars; body ≤ 4,000 chars; single pass, never rescan; runs last (§4.10 stage 11).
- **R2 rows closed:** C-69, C-70, C-71, C-73, D-53
- **R4 parts:** aho-corasick (adopt); OpenWhispr `src/utils/snippets.ts` (MIT, port); espanso-render (GPL, reference only for future variables).
- **Handy files touched:** none beyond the `deliver` hook.
- **New fork files:** `fork/db/migrations/0004_snippets.sql` (incl. nullable `body_html`), `fork/text/snippets.rs`, `src/fork/snippets/{SnippetsPage,SnippetDialog}.tsx`.
- **Tests:** the Wispr spec as an acceptance table (≥ 40 cases); Unicode boundaries; snippet bodies never pass through the LLM.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** a three-line address snippet in Mail, Slack, Notes.
- **Exit criteria:** acceptance table green; Snippets page counts update live.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-11, S-13, S-16 (shared import wizard).
- **As built (2026-10-31), decisions that change the plan above** (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Snippets (S-17)"; real-app evidence in `docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-S-17.md`):
  - **Files:** migration `0006_snippets.sql` (0004 was S-16's dictionary, 0005 S-18's notifications; the `snippets` table itself was in 0001): `snippets.last_hit_at`, `snippets.source` (`user`/`import`), `entry_meta.inserted_text`. The matcher is `fork/text/snippets.rs` (+ `snippets_tests.rs`); the store, CSV import/export and validation are a new top-level module `fork/snippets/{mod,store,import,export,tests}.rs` (added to §4.1); commands `fork/commands/snippets.rs` (8 `fork_snippets_*`, event `ForkSnippetsChanged`); frontend `src/fork/snippets/{SnippetsPage,SnippetDialog,ImportWizard,useSnippets}`. No Handy file touched (stage 11 lives inside `fork::text::finish`, already the first line of `deliver`).
  - **Port:** OpenWhispr `src/utils/snippets.ts` (MIT, commit fc786f41) re-expressed on `aho-corasick` with S-11's byte-mapped fold, plus Turkish İ/ı → i and NFC (`icu_normalizer` 2.1 without default features, already compiled; Cargo.toml budget 19 → 20). File marked `MIT AND GPL-3.0-or-later`, NOTICE §3 and PORTS.md rows. espanso was not used.
  - **Matching:** alone = the whole take's letters and digits (folded) equal the trigger's: punctuation, symbols, spacing and case ignored, the take becomes exactly the saved text. Inline = the trigger as written in any case; it must not touch a letter, digit, `_` or combining mark (no boundary next to scripts without spaces); an apostrophe after it is allowed only for `'s` (possessive kept after the text); "n't", "'ll", "'re" … do not match; an apostrophe between letters on the left ("O'Brien") blocks. Overlaps: the longest trigger by characters, then the earlier, then the older snippet. One pass over the original text; inserted text is never scanned. A sentence mark right after an inline match is dropped when the saved text ends with the same mark. Two triggers that differ only by punctuation can coexist; alone, the older wins.
  - **Order:** after the LLM inside `fork::text::finish`: 9 → 12 → 11. S-12a (landed while S-17 was built) runs its own stage 9 right after the LLM through the new `fork::text::after_llm` (9 → 12, no snippets), so snippets still expand once, in `deliver`, after History saved the spoken words. Stage 12 runs before the snippets so triggers meet tidy spacing and the saved text keeps its own spacing and blank lines (nothing normalizes after it). A unit test fails if an upstream merge moves Handy's LLM call after `insert::deliver` or `text::finish` out of `deliver`'s first line; another shows the LLM input carries the trigger, never the text.
  - **Limits:** trigger ≤ 60 characters on one line with at least one letter or digit; text ≤ 4,000 characters, plain text, `\r\n` → `\n`, tabs allowed, other control characters refused, trailing whitespace and leading blank lines dropped; 1,000 snippets and 1,000 import rows (Wispr's import limit); duplicate key = the trigger NFC-tidied and folded like the matcher.
  - **History and stats:** Handy's row and `entry_meta.final_text` keep the spoken words (stats count those: a long saved text never inflates words or WPM); `entry_meta.inserted_text` has what was pasted (for S-15b's Copy); `text_stages` gets `{"stage":"snippets","changes":n}`; the paste outcome follows the rekeyed text. Hits and the history update are written off the paste thread. Logs carry counts only.
  - **Page:** no tabs (sharing is out of scope): a live count line ("N snippets · used N times"), search over trigger and text (case and accent blind), sort newest/oldest/A-Z/Z-A/most used, refresh, banner `snippets.examples` with three examples of our own, rows with up to three lines of the text and "Used N times", edit (keeps the use count), delete with Undo. The dialog's text field keeps Return for new lines; ⌘Return saves. Import/Export reuse S-16's wizard and reader (`fork_dictionary_read_file`), format in `docs/fork/user/import-format.md` › Snippets.
  - **Translations:** 119 new `fork` keys machine-translated with the `llama-server` backend (Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507), flagged `machine`; the placeholder page's 3 keys removed.
  - **Real app (M5 Max, portable profile `com.wisprbro.s17check`, Parakeet TDT 0.6B v3, `say` through the speakers):** snippet added through the dialog; alone and inline into TextEdit, a Safari WebKit editor page and a Chrome 156 chat page (typing path, nothing sent): 14/15 exact (one TextEdit read-back came back empty while the app logged the paste confirmed and stored the right text; three re-runs passed); the page showed "used 12 times" live; clipboard byte-identical after the runs.
  - **Not verified:** Mail, Slack and Notes themselves (stand-ins above), a person's voice, VoiceOver, Finder drag and the Export save panel by a person, a real LLM rewrite before stage 11 (S-12a).

### S-18 Notifications

- **Goal:** a local inbox plus a few macOS notifications.
- **User-visible outcome:** the bell opens a panel (filter, mark all read, empty state "Nothing new"); entries for model ready, update ready, shortcut problems, crash reports, milestones (streaks, word counts) and tips; a one-time popup for a new feature per version; macOS notifications only for model ready, update ready, queued takes ready and errors needing action, with action buttons where useful. Toggles: Suggestions, Announcements, Milestones. Esc dismisses toasts (observed, not blocked).
- **R2 rows closed:** C-17, C-201, C-202, C-91, E-91
- **R4 parts:** `objc2-user-notifications` 0.3.2 (in tree): `UNUserNotificationCenter` authorization at the first real need, one category with a "Copy latest" action, a delegate that routes the click and the action (RS-10: tauri-plugin-notification 2.4.0 has no click or action support on macOS desktop and is not adopted).
- **Handy files touched:** ⚠ `Cargo.toml` (fork block: the `objc2-user-notifications` features, if not already enabled); the delegate is installed from `fork::on_ready`. No notification plugin and no capability change (RS-10).
- **New fork files:** `fork/notify.rs`, `fork/db/migrations/0005_notifications.sql`, `src/fork/hub/NotificationsPanel.tsx`, `src/fork/hub/FeaturePopup.tsx`.
- **Tests:** inbox CRUD; toggles filter creation; macOS notification only for the four kinds; click and action buttons route to the right page or action.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** RS-10 notification items on the real slice.
- **Exit criteria:** no notification permission prompt before the first real need.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-13, RS-10.
- **As built (2026-10-31):** decisions that refine this block (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Notifications (S-18)"; real-Mac evidence in `docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-S-18.md`):
- **Mac wave 1 fix (2026-11-03, `fix/wave1-b`):** the one-time feature popup no longer covers S-22's View changes diff (Finding 9). `src/fork/hub/featurePopupGate.ts` defers it (never dismisses it) while a take runs (Handy's `started`/`locked` until 1.5 s after `stopped`/`cancelled`), while `#/hub/transforms/changes` is open, while any other `role=dialog`/`alertdialog` is in the page (What's New, Fix shortcut, page dialogs, the notifications panel), and for 0.8 s after the page loads so a handoff arriving with the page lands first. Playwright: `waits while View changes is open, then shows` and `waits while a take runs or another dialog is open`.
  - **Files:** `fork/notify.rs` plus `fork/notify/{policy,catalog,inbox,macos,tests}.rs` (a `notify.rs` root with submodules keeps §4.1's top-level name), `fork/commands/notifications.rs`, migration `0005_notifications.sql` as planned (the table was in 0001; S-18 adds only `dedupe_key` with a partial unique index), `src/fork/hub/{NotificationsPanel,FeaturePopup}.tsx` and `notifications.ts` (store and text), `src/fork/debug/NotificationsDebug.tsx`, `scripts/wb-s18-notifications.swift` (Mac test helper). No Handy file touched except the `Cargo.toml` fork block (`objc2-user-notifications` without default features, `block2`; both already in the lockfile, +4 on the `Cargo.toml` budget). No plugin, no capability: the §4.1 "`notification:default` from S-18" entry is dropped.
  - **macOS policy:** only model ready, update ready, queued takes ready and shortcut problems (the error needing action today) can reach macOS, each behind its toggle and the master `notifications.system`, and **only while no app window has focus**; in front, the entry is an in-app toast instead. Authorization is requested inside the first delivery (`getNotificationSettings` → `NotDetermined` → `requestAuthorization` with Alert, Sound and ProvidesAppNotificationSettings), so launch, onboarding (the first model download happens with the window in front) and inbox-only kinds never prompt. Shortcut problems are posted only once Handy's onboarding is done.
  - **Toggles (C-91):** `NotificationSettings` gained `suggestions`, `announcements`, `milestones` (serde defaults true, no schema bump); they filter at creation. They live in the panel's ⋯ menu with "Mark all as read" and "Mac notifications" until S-14's System › Notifications lists them too.
  - **Producers wired now:** Handy's `model-download-complete` (no hook; the language detector is ignored), the hotkey tap failing to start (`fork::shortcut::start`; Accessibility button when `AXIsProcessTrusted` is false), the newest crash file under 7 days (click reveals it in Finder; this was S-27's line in `crash.rs`), a recreated `fork.db` (`report_db_recovered`), milestones (words 1k…1M, streaks 3…365 days, highest unposted only, each streak length once per streak) and tips (5 tips for built features, by dictation count, at most one a day) after `ForkStatsUpdated`, and the version's feature announcement. Queued takes ready (S-07c) and update ready (S-28) have their notices, buttons and tests but no producer yet.
  - **Actions:** one `activate` path for inbox clicks, inbox buttons, toasts, macOS clicks and macOS buttons; a button an entry does not offer falls back to its click. Buttons: queued takes → Copy latest (background, no window) and Open; shortcut problem → Open Settings (Accessibility pane). A macOS click shows the Hub and emits `ForkNotificationOpened`, which navigates; "Notification Settings" in System Settings opens the panel.
  - **Feature popup (C-202):** Handy's `WhatsNewGate` stays (its notes are Handy's); `FeaturePopup` shows the current version's entry of `notify::catalog::FEATURES` once (`feature.<id>` in `dismissed_banners`), hidden when Announcements are off. 1.0.0-alpha.1 announces the inbox itself.
  - **Esc:** a `keydown` listener in the Hub and an Esc-registry observer (`ForkToastsDismiss`) close toasts; neither stops the key.
  - **Translations:** 64 new keys machine-translated with the `llama-server` backend (Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507), the worst literal results corrected by hand; all flagged `machine` for S-37. Rust renders plurals with English rules (`_one`/`_other`); the inbox re-resolves them with i18next per language.
- **Real Mac (M5 Max, macOS 26.6.2, ad-hoc release bundle "Wispr Bro S18 Check" in `~/Applications`, portable profile):** no prompt at launch, in onboarding-free Hub use or for inbox-only kinds; the first model-ready post with another app in front asked (logged as the first real need). Don't Allow → inbox only, never asked again; Allow → 5/5 delivered, buttons registered (Copy latest, Open, Open Settings); clicks opened Dictation and Settings › About, Copy latest put the latest dictation on the clipboard with no window, Open opened Settings › History, Open Settings opened the Accessibility pane; with the Hub in front new entries were toasts and Esc closed them (`docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-S-18.md`). Learned: usernoted refuses a bundle under `/tmp` ("not allowed"); a debug build cannot start under `open` (Handy's bindings export panics on `/`); `--start-hidden` reports a focused window (fixed: visible and focused). **Needs the Developer ID build (S-18.2):** the real identifier, launch through LaunchServices with its own TCC grants, a click after the app quit (relaunch → page), "Notification Settings" from System Settings, macOS 15 and 13.

### S-07c Offline queue and late delivery (built after S-18)

- **Goal:** takes recorded with no usable model are never lost and never overwrite the clipboard.
- **User-visible outcome:** on an offline first launch (or when the model is missing and cannot download), presses keep recording; each take appears in History as "Waiting for speech model". When a model arrives, the takes are transcribed in order and one notification says "3 dictations ready" with **Copy latest** and **Open**. ⌃⌘V pastes the latest. If the onboarding window was closed mid-download, the inbox and a macOS notification say when dictation is ready.
- **R2 rows closed:** B-210
- **R4 parts:** Handy `managers/transcription_jobs.rs` (MIT, reuse) for the transcription queue; R4 "Bootstrap model" row queued-audio pattern (W-22).
- **Handy files touched:** none beyond the S-07a mic-gate hook.
- **New fork files:** `fork/models/queue.rs` (durable `queued_takes`, survives relaunch, ordered processing), `src/fork/history/WaitingRow.tsx`.
- **Tests:** offline first launch → 3 takes → relaunch → model arrives → 3 History rows, one notification, **clipboard unchanged** until Copy; Copy latest writes only the latest; failed transcription stays in History with Retry.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** offline first launch with networking off, 3 takes, quit, relaunch online; notification action copies the latest.
- **Exit criteria:** 0 lost queued takes across relaunch; clipboard untouched by delivery.
- **Effort / depends on:** S / S-07a, S-09, S-15b, S-18.
- **As built (2026-10-31):** `fork/models/queue.rs` replaces S-07a's session-only queue with durable `queued_takes` recovery, orphaned-WAV History reconstruction, ordered Handy transcription jobs and failed-take Retry. `src/fork/history/WaitingRow.tsx` shows **Waiting for speech model**. Late delivery updates History and posts one batch notification/inbox item with Copy latest and Open; it never pastes, shows a card or writes the clipboard, and Copy latest writes only the newest dictation. Closing onboarding does not suppress the ready notification. Quit now cancels active HF downloads and waits for hf-hub to commit its resume offset, so relaunch resumes the partial artifact where ranges are supported; checksum and partial-file validation remain enforced. No Handy file changed. Full Rust suite (1,312 passed, 30 ignored), focused model/History/notification tests, History Playwright (21 passed), build, lint, format, guards and binding checks passed on the slice branch. **Real Mac (2026-11-01, `docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-backend-wave-S-07c-S-19b-S-20.md`):** three offline takes survived quit and transcribed in order; one batch notification, Copy latest, clipboard unchanged until explicit Copy, failed-take Retry and ranged quit/resume with checksum all passed. A follow-up lets native Open mount History without completing or changing unfinished onboarding; a fresh real-notification rerun passed. The strengthened download-resume regression passed 100 consecutive runs.

Styles apply in English only (cleanup level applies to all languages). Optional per-app "Press Enter after dictating" (off by default; the pill shows "sends automatically" when it is on; never after a guard fallback, an unverified paste or a card).

- **Handy files touched:** none beyond hooks.
- **New fork files:** `fork/context/app_rules.rs`, `fork/db/migrations/0006_app_rules.sql`, `fork/text/style_rules.rs`, `src/fork/style/{StylePage,GroupTab,PresetCard,AppPicker,CleanupTab}.tsx`.

- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-05, S-09, S-12a, S-13.
- **As built (2026-10-31), decisions that refine this block** (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Styles: app groups and presets (S-19a)"; evidence in `docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-S-19a.md`):
  - **Files:** as planned, plus `fork/context/apps.rs` (installed apps and Finder icons), `fork/commands/style.rs` (`fork_style_*`, `ForkAppRulesChanged`), `src/fork/style/{AppIcon.tsx,presets.ts,useStyle.ts}`; the migration is **0007** (0006 went to S-17). It rebuilds v1's empty `app_rules`: `style_group` may be NULL (the built-in group stays; the row only carries auto-send), plus `kind` (`app`/`host`, so S-19b needs no migration), `app_name`, `updated_at`; `source` is always `user` (the built-in table stays `categories.json`). No Handy file is touched: everything runs in S-12a's cleanup step and S-04's delivery.
  - **Precedence:** BetterWispr `WritingMode` tiers, ported (GPL, VoiceInk-derived): a site rule (host, subdomain, path prefix; matched already, S-19b supplies the page) → the user's app rule → `categories.json`. `entry_meta.style_group` now records the group with the user's rules. Trigger words and per-mode levels are not ported.
  - **Presets are deterministic (stage 10, BetterWispr `StyleFormatter`, Apache-2.0):** Formal = the cleaned text; Relaxed drops commas (not inside numbers, brackets, backtick code or at a line end) and the final period; Very relaxed also lowers sentence starts (not "iPhone", "NASA" or dictionary words); Upbeat turns the final period into "!". BetterWispr's greeting join is not ported (no stage may remove a `\n`). **No preset changes the cleanup prompt:** an Upbeat fragment of our own ("exclamation marks on friendly sentences") was built and measured on the S-12a eval and cost guard fallbacks (Light "language kept" 98.3 %, below the 99 % gate), so the brief's "RS-07 gates must not regress" ruled it out; `prompt::style_fragment` keeps the slot and the snapshot test pins that every preset composes to the level's prompt. R2 D-24's "needs the LLM" is therefore not met: Upbeat only ends the text with "!".
  - **When a style applies:** dictations only (retries and file jobs go to History), the target = the frontmost app at cleanup time (as the AI-chat skip), English only (the recognizer's language, else the guess from the spoken languages, else Handy's `lang_id`; too short to tell = English unless the user speaks only other languages), never in AI-chat apps; also at level None and after a cleanup fallback (punctuation and case only, never words).
  - **Auto-send:** per app, off by default, from the app chip's menu ("Press Enter after dictating"). Armed per take (keyed by the final text) only when the cleanup did not fall back; the paste sends plain Return only after a `confirmed` paste (new `deliver::Request::submit_strict`) into the same app; never after `unverified`, a card or a fallback. Handy's global `auto_submit` keeps its own rule. The bar shows "Sends automatically" (with a Return icon) in the microphone label's place at the take start.
  - **Page:** tabs Personal messages / Work messages / Email / Other / Cleanup (the tab is the hub section: `navigate("style", "email")`); app chips with icons and a menu (move, remove = to Other, back to the usual group, Undo toast, auto-send); "+" opens a searchable list of installed apps (`/Applications` and friends plus running apps); the group lists the user's rules, the built-in table's installed apps and the installed apps dictated into. Before/after examples are our own English sample texts (`presets.ts`, `lang="en"`, the same in every locale), checked against the rules by a Rust test. Cleanup tab = S-12a's `CleanupLevelPicker` (still also on the debug panel).
  - **Eval (S-12a replay, shipped path, owner languages):** no style identical to S-12a (Light damage 0.0 %, answer 0.0 %, filler 95.0 %, language 100 %, catch 100 %; Medium damage 0.0 %); each preset applied to that run (`eval_restyle`): every gate unchanged. RS-07 Medium/style thresholds hold.
  - **Real app (test identity, TTS, TextEdit standing in for each group through a user rule):** Very relaxed, Upbeat, Relaxed and Formal on real takes after a real cleanup; a Dutch take unstyled; `entry_meta.style_group` follows the rule; auto-send pressed Return after a confirmed paste and not after a forced cleanup timeout; the page in the real app lists Messages (Personal) and Mail (Email) with their icons, moved TextEdit with its menu and turned auto-send on, "Add app" lists the installed apps.
  - **Not verified:** takes into Slack (not installed), Messages and Mail themselves (the owner's data); the native bar's "Sends automatically" on screen; a person's voice; VoiceOver; site rules on a real page (S-19b).

### S-19b Styles: web-app detection

- **Goal:** Gmail in a browser counts as Email, detected locally.
- **User-visible outcome:** web apps in Chrome, Arc, Brave, Edge and Safari get the style of their group (Gmail → Email, Slack web → Work); the Style page lists sites alongside apps.
- **R2 rows closed:** D-22
- **R4 parts:** mado 0.0.21 `BrowserURLExtractor.swift` + `SupportedBrowsers.swift` from the crates.io tarball (MIT, port; never `WebsiteInfo.swift`); BetterWispr `WebsiteReader.swift` (GPL, port fallback); BetterWispr host patterns (GPL, port); Tambourine origin-only reduction (AGPL, port idea).
- **Handy files touched:** none.
- **New fork files:** `fork/context/url.rs`, host rules in `fork/context/categories.json`.
- **Tests:** host matching precedence over the browser app; host-only storage; window-title fallback.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** RS-04 URL rows on the real slice; Gmail in Chrome/Arc/Safari → Email.
- **Exit criteria:** host detected in the five browsers or the title fallback used; only the host is stored.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-19a, RS-04.
- **As built (2026-10-31):** `fork/context/url.rs` uses the existing AX thread for local Chrome, Arc, Brave, Edge and Safari detection, with mado document/address-bar extraction (MIT) and BetterWispr web-area fallbacks (GPL), retaining their copyright and modification notices. URLs are reduced immediately to a host; paths, queries, fragments, userinfo, ports, page text and window titles are never persisted. Focused or indeterminate address bars are rejected, and title fallback returns only a known Gmail/Slack host. Built-in host rules map Gmail to Email and Slack web to Work and take precedence over browser app rules; `entry_meta.url_host` records only the reduced host. The Style page lists sites beside apps with the existing move/remove/reset/undo and auto-send controls. Full `cargo test` (1,320 passed, 30 ignored), lint, build, format, guards and Playwright (270 passed, 10 skipped) passed on the slice branch. **Real Mac (2026-11-01, same backend-wave report):** Gmail and Slack web in Chrome and public pages in Safari selected the right group over a conflicting browser rule, known-title fallback worked, and only reduced hosts reached storage or logs. **Not verified:** real extraction in Arc, Brave and Edge, which are not installed on the test Mac.

### S-20 Context awareness

- **Goal:** spell names and terms right using text near the cursor, locally.
- **User-visible outcome:** on by default; a small "context" glyph appears on the pill while processing when nearby text was used; the History row says "Context used" (never the text). Off switch in Data and Privacy.
- **Rules (Wispr exclusions):** never read password, secure, numeric or URL-bar fields, terminals, password managers or banking/finance apps (bundle list); budget ≤ 1,500 characters before/selected/after the caret; ≤ 100 ms or skip; never stored, cleared when the take ends; used only as spelling hints for the ASR vocabulary (where the model supports it) and the LLM prompt.
- **R2 rows closed:** D-58
- **R4 parts:** SpeakoFlow caret window (MIT, in S-05 layer); FluidVoice `DictationContextCapture.swift` budgeted walk + password-manager skip list (GPL-3.0, port); Wispr docs 4678293671 (behaviour).
- **New fork files:** `fork/context/caret.rs`, `fork/context/exclusions.json`, `src/fork/pill/states/ContextGlyph.tsx`, `eval/context/names.jsonl` (30 items: an unusual name visible near the caret, spoken in the take).
- **Tests:** exclusion list; budget and timeout; nothing persisted (DB and logs checked); prompt includes hints only when allowed.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** dictate a colleague's unusual surname visible in a Slack thread; verify the spelling and that the text never hits disk.
- **Exit criteria:** on the 30-item name set, correct-spelling rate improves by ≥ 20 percentage points with context on vs off; added p95 latency ≤ 100 ms.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-05, S-12a.
- **As built (2026-10-31; recovered from the interrupted slice agent and integrated with S-19a by the orchestrator):** `fork/context/{awareness,caret,hints}.rs` + `exclusions.json` (FluidVoice `DictationContextCapture.swift` port, GPL file: privacy checks, bounded walk, visible-range-only reads, password-manager markers), `fork/context/names_eval.rs` (the 30-item name eval), `fork/llm/prompts/context.txt` (our own CONTEXT fragment), `src/fork/pill/states/ContextGlyph.tsx`, `eval/context/names.jsonl`, `scripts/wb-s20/`, `tests/fork/pill-context.spec.ts`. Flow per take (DEC-24): record start queues one budgeted AX read (never waits) → key-up arms the slot → hints feed the recognizer's vocabulary (`managers/transcription.rs` hook), the spelling stage (`fork::text::process`) and the cleanup prompt (`Request.context`; `keep_with_context` adds near-caret names to the entity guard so a name the take already spells right must survive) → any use marks the take (pill glyph, History "Context used", never the text) → the slot clears when the take ends (120 s armed TTL). Exclusions: Secure Input, password managers (bundle markers), banking/finance (bundle ids, markers incl. segments like `mobilebanking`, hosts), terminals, numeric/URL-bar/secure fields; own app skipped. Nothing is written to disk or logged beyond counts, times and skip reasons. Off switch in fork settings. Integration fixes while merging with S-19a: `post_process::Request` and `prompt::Spec` carry both `style` and `context`; `run` notes context use before S-19a's stage 10 and auto-send arming; the eval runner keeps one `system_prompt_*` pair with both fields. **Real Mac (2026-11-01, `docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-backend-wave-S-07c-S-19b-S-20.md`):** the 30-item eval improved correct spelling by 40.0 percentage points with 1.966 ms added p95; real context reads had 53.5 ms p95. The unusual-surname correction, clipboard, nearby-text non-persistence and Terminal exclusion passed. Card number initially failed when generic focus bypassed the sensitive subtree. Follow-up fixes restrict direct caret reads to text-entry/search fields, make the whole-window walk inspect skipped/offscreen/failed-batch subtrees, reject aggregate `AXWebArea` values, and refuse browser chrome when no page is inspectable. The isolated Chrome harness enables renderer accessibility and creates the Card input only for `#card`. **Real-Mac rerun PASS:** Card number returned `sensitive (0 chars, 0 hints)` in 1.4 ms; ordinary Chrome returned `read ok (448 chars, 8 hints)` in 25.1 ms, and both clipboards stayed byte-identical. Slack itself, a real colleague and VoiceOver remain person checks.

### S-21 Transforms core

- **Goal:** rewrite selected text in any app with a hotkey.
- **User-visible outcome:** with Transforms on (the fork setting, toggled on the fork debug panel until S-22 adds the page toggle), select text anywhere and press ⌥1 (Polish) or ⌥2 (Prompt Engineer): the text is replaced in place, ⌘Z undoes it, and the pill shows "Done · View changes". With nothing selected, the transform uses the field contents, else the last dictation from the past 60 s, else the pill says "Select text first".
- **R2 rows closed:** D-29, D-30, D-31, D-32, D-37, D-201, D-202, D-203, D-204
- **R4 parts:** selection chain: AX (S-05), VoiceInk text markers (GPL, port), SelectedTextKit menu Edit › Copy via AX (MIT, port), SpeakoFlow `selection.rs` ⌘C sentinel (MIT, port); MacParakeet `SelectionCaptureService`/`SelectionReplacementService` tests (GPL, port as spec); OpenLess `selection.rs` (AGPL, reference); FreeFlow `commandModeSystemPrompt` (MIT, port); `fork/llm` engine (S-12a).
- **Handy files touched:** none beyond hooks.
- **New fork files:** `fork/transforms/{mod,selection,replace,run,record}.rs`, `fork/db/migrations/0007_transforms.sql`.
- **Rules:** replace by clipboard paste (one ⌘Z step); abort if the frontmost app or element changed (D-203); refuse selections > 6,000 characters with a pill message (D-204); Esc cancels; keep the last 20 runs only. Bound ⌥ keys swallow their characters while Transforms are on (RS-11: ⌥1 ¡, ⌥2 ™, ⌥O ø, 0/25 typed), so the Transforms toggle (S-22) says which characters the shortcuts replace and offers ⌃⌥1/⌃⌥2/⌃⌥O in one click (RS-11: with those bound, ⌥ characters type 25/25). Fork bindings are never also registered in Handy's manager: the tap created last swallows a key before the other sees it.
- **Tests:** selection chain fallbacks with fakes; focus guard; no-selection rules; record bounding; hotkeys registered only when Transforms are on (DEC-31).
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** RS-04 replace rows + RS-11 ⌥ characters on the real slice; Polish in Gmail, Slack, VS Code, Notes.
- **Exit criteria:** §5.1 Transform latency; ⌘Z restores the original in the 10 apps.
- **Effort / depends on:** L / S-04, S-05, S-12a, RS-04, RS-11.
- **As built (2026-11-01):** `fork/transforms/{mod,selection,run,replace,record}.rs` implements conditional fork-only ⌥1/⌥2 registration, AX → text markers → Edit Copy → sentineled ⌘C selection capture, field/recent-dictation fallbacks, the 6,000-character and focus-change guards, Esc cancellation, a 2.8 s generation deadline and bounded run history. Replacement reuses S-04's serialized clipboard line for exactly one paste and one-step undo. Migration `0009_transforms.sql` seeds Polish and Prompt Engineer without changing earlier migrations; the existing schema keeps the last 20 runs. The debug panel has the opt-in toggle, RS-11 character warning and one-click ⌃⌥ alternatives; the pill emits the View changes handoff for S-22. SelectedTextKit's MIT notice is recorded. Integrated gates with S-14: 1,343 Rust tests and 296 Playwright/10 skipped passed, plus guards, ESLint, key parity and formatting. The ten new strings are key-complete but copy-English/`untranslated` pending the transform-wave machine batch. **Not run:** real selection/replacement and ⌥/⌃⌥ behavior, Polish in Gmail/Slack/VS Code/Notes, 10-app ⌘Z, hardware latency, clipboard fidelity and native View changes; these are queued for the S-21/22/23 Mac wave. **Mac wave 1 (2026-11-02):** Polish on a TextEdit selection PASS (1.1-1.3 s, one ⌘Z restores, clipboard unchanged, ⌥1 swallowed); Gmail/Slack/VS Code/Notes, RS-04 rows and 10-app undo still not run (apps/accounts) (`docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-macwave-1.md`).

### S-22 Transforms page, editor, diff and auto-apply

- **Goal:** Wispr's Transforms page and diff view.
- **User-visible outcome:** Transforms page with an opt-in toggle, banner "Rewrite selected text in any app" (its "Try it" and "How it works" buttons appear when S-31 registers the lesson), "Apply after every dictation" with a chosen transform, My Transforms (Polish, Prompt Engineer, Create your own), Reset to defaults, Create new. The Polish modal: hotkey, description, example with a preview button, Autosave, Reset, rule toggles (Shorter, Clearer wording, Better order, Add headings and lists, Keep my tone), custom prompt, writing examples (paste or load .txt/.md; up to 3 × 1,500 chars). ⌥O or the pill action opens a diff card: "N changes", removed words struck, additions highlighted, Configure, Copy, 👍/👎 (stored locally), Retry.
- **R2 rows closed:** D-28, D-33, D-34, D-35, D-36, D-38, D-40, D-41, D-42, D-205, D-206
- **R4 parts:** similar 3.2.1 (Apache-2.0, adopt) word diff with `_deadline`; Radix (adopt); prompt builder (S-12a).
- **New fork files:** `fork/text/diff.rs`, `src/fork/transforms/{TransformsPage,TransformCard,TransformModal,DiffCard,RulesList,ExamplesEditor}.tsx`, `src/fork/pill/states/DiffCard.tsx` (W-16).
- **Rules:** auto-apply runs after cleanup through the same engine (DEC-13); on failure the original is pasted; runs closer than 5 s apart skip auto-apply.
- **Tests:** diff ops snapshot; rules → prompt fragments; custom transform CRUD; auto-apply fail-open.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** ⌥O after a transform in Slack; auto-apply on a 3-sentence dictation.
- **Exit criteria:** R1 §4/§10 Transforms behaviours present (lesson entry points from S-31).
- **Effort / depends on:** L / S-21, S-13.
- **As built (2026-11-02):** `src/fork/transforms/` implements the opt-in page, banner, auto-apply choice, built-in/custom cards, Reset/Create new and the full editor (hotkey, description, preview, Autosave, five rules, custom prompt and three bounded writing examples). `fork/transforms/{store,auto}.rs` adds custom CRUD, rule/example prompt fragments and fail-open/throttled post-cleanup application using the existing schema—no DB migration. `fork/text/diff.rs` uses the existing LCS for a deadline-bounded word diff; main-window and pill DiffCards implement the S-21 handoff/⌥O with changes, Configure, Copy, local ratings and Retry. Fork settings v4 adds the View changes default without replacing user/unset bindings. Integrated gates: 1,353 Rust tests and 305 Playwright/10 skipped passed, including diff snapshots, CRUD, auto-apply, keyboard, axe, RTL and reduced motion; guards, ESLint, translation parity and formatting passed. One existing notification milestone test failed once in the first parallel full run, then passed alone and in the full rerun. The 83 new locale strings are key-complete/machine-flagged pending the transform-wave translation batch. **Not run:** real Slack ⌥O diff and a real three-sentence auto-apply/fail-open take; queued with the transform Mac wave. **Mac wave 1 (2026-11-02):** ⌥O opened View changes after a TextEdit Polish (PASS, Slack not installed); the three-sentence auto-apply take pasted unchanged with cleanup timing out at load ~96 and no auto-apply run logged: not verified (`docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-macwave-1.md`).

### S-23 Command Mode

- **Goal:** speak an instruction to edit or write text.
- **User-visible outcome:** with Command Mode on (Transforms page card), hold fn+Ctrl and say "make this friendlier": the selection is rewritten. With no selection, the instruction uses the field contents as context and inserts the result at the caret (writing tasks such as "reply saying I'll be late"). With no text and no selection, the pill says "Command Mode edits and writes text. Select text or click into a field." It never looks things up. With Command Mode off, fn+Ctrl is plain dictation (DEC-31).
- **R2 rows closed:** D-44, D-45
- **R4 parts:** FreeFlow `commandModeSystemPrompt` (MIT, port); `fork/transforms` (S-21); S-12a guards with an instruction-mode profile.
- **New fork files:** `fork/transforms/command.rs`, `src/fork/transforms/CommandModeCard.tsx`, `eval/command/{items.jsonl,RUBRIC.md}` (50 commands; 3-point rubric: 2 = did exactly what was asked with no damage, 1 = right intent with a minor flaw, 0 = wrong, damaging or answered instead of edited).
- **Tests:** chord routing from S-01 (on and off); three context cases; output bounds; guard profile.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** 20 commands across Mail, Slack, Docs; ⌘Z restores.
- **Exit criteria:** on the 50-command set, two reviewers score independently with the rubric; ≥ 90 % of items score ≥ 1 from both and ≥ 70 % score 2 from both.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-21.
- **As built (2026-11-02):** `fork/transforms/command.rs` adds the FreeFlow MIT prompt port, instruction-mode guards, 2.8 s deadline, output bounds and S-21 selection/replacement reuse. The independently opt-in `CommandModeCard.tsx` and schema-v5 `fn+Ctrl` binding route only marked takes to Command Mode; off remains plain dictation. Selection rewrites replace the selection, field-only writing inserts at the caret, and empty input shows local guidance without a model call. Command takes bypass ordinary cleanup/auto-apply and are excluded from dictation stats. `eval/command/{items.jsonl,RUBRIC.md}` contains 50 synthetic items and the independent-review protocol. Automated gates: 1,361 Rust tests/31 ignored and 306 Playwright/10 skipped passed, plus guards, ESLint, translation parity and formatting. All 50 eval items ran through the shipped generation path in 12.68 s; 48 returned accepted output, but these counts are **not rubric acceptance**. No independent reviewers ran, and one no-lookup sample invented weather; product-quality/reviewer criteria remain open. Nine new strings are copy-English/`untranslated` pending the transform-wave batch. **Not run:** independent two-reviewer scoring and 20 real commands across Mail/Slack/Docs with ⌘Z. **Mac wave 1 (2026-11-02):** blocked: Command Mode accepts only fn+Ctrl, which the owner's running Wispr Bro also holds, and Mail/Slack/Docs need the owner's accounts.

### S-24 Scratchpad

- **Goal:** Wispr's Scratchpad: quick notes you can dictate into.
- **User-visible outcome:** Scratchpad page with "Add to the bar", a shortcut button (dialog proposes ⌃⌥S, because a bare ⌥S would swallow "ß" (RS-11); unset by default), "New note", Recents (edit, pin, delete) and search. A small floating note window with tabs, +, expand, close, a left rail (sidebar, new note, search, ✨ Polish, text size), the text area and Copy. With "Add to the bar" on, the resting bar becomes a group: Dictate and Scratchpad (Notetaker joins in M2); hovering each shows a tooltip ("Hold fn to dictate", "Scratchpad"). Open behaviour: resume last note or new note.
- **R2 rows closed:** A-03, C-74, C-75, C-77, C-78, C-216, D-209
- **Regression protected (HAVE):** C-76
- **R4 parts:** tauri-nspanel / Tauri window (adopt); FTS5 trigram (in tree); `fork/transforms` for ✨ (S-21).
- **Handy files touched:** none (the `fork-scratchpad` page is added to `src/fork/vite-inputs.ts`).
- **New fork files:** `fork/scratchpad/{mod,window,store}.rs`, `fork/db/migrations/0008_notes.sql`, `src/fork/scratchpad/{ScratchpadPage,NoteWindow,NoteTabs}.tsx`, capability entry `fork-scratchpad`, `fork/pill/slots.rs`.
- **Tests:** autosave (debounced 500 ms, survives crash), tabs, search, pin, slot API with tooltips (A-03), window capability.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** dictate into the note window; open via ⌥S after binding; note survives force-quit.
- **Exit criteria:** R1 §10 Scratchpad behaviours present.
- **Effort / depends on:** L / S-03, S-13, S-21, F-06.
- **As built (2026-11-02):** `fork/scratchpad/{mod,store,window}.rs` and `fork/commands/scratchpad.rs` implement transactional notes/contentless trigram FTS, CRUD/search, resume/new-note behavior, the open shortcut and singleton floating window; Polish reuses S-21 without AX/paste side effects. `src/fork/scratchpad/` provides the Hub Recents page, unset Ctrl+Option+S proposal, tabs, rail tools, 500 ms autosave, text size, Copy and expand/close. `fork/pill/{slots,native,panel}.rs` adds independent native Dictate/Scratchpad buttons with accessible names and tooltips. The existing `0001` notes/FTS tables suffice, so there is no DB migration; fork settings v6 migrates the earlier location choice to resume-last and defaults the bar slot off. The standalone window has an explicit fork capability. Integrated gates: 1,367 Rust tests/31 ignored and 312 Playwright/10 skipped passed, plus build, guards, ESLint, translation parity and formatting. The 57 new strings are copy-English/`untranslated` pending the next wave. **Not run:** native note dictation, bound-shortcut opening, force-quit recovery, and grouped-bar visuals/tooltips/VoiceOver; queued for the UI Mac wave. **Mac wave 1 (2026-11-02):** the note window loaded the Hub (wrong page path), fixed on `fix/macwave-s24`; with the fix ⌥S opens the note and a note survives `kill -9` (PASS). FAIL: the app loses focus within 0.2 s of a take started from the note, so dictating into it does not work; ⌥S does not focus the note (`docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-macwave-1.md`).
- **Mac wave 1 fixes (2026-11-03, `fix/wave1-b`):** (1) **S-24.1, the note lost focus at take start.** Cause: the Flow Bar's per-take web view was built with tauri-nspanel's `no_activate(true)`, which switches the whole app to the `Prohibited` activation policy while it builds the panel; with Wispr Bro's own window in front that deactivates the app and macOS gives focus back to the previous app (0.2 s after SIGUSR2 in the wave). Removed: the panel is built hidden and unfocused, so tao never activates for it, and the non-activating style mask plus `_setPreventsActivation:` still cover clicks (test `no_fork_panel_switches_the_app_activation_policy` fails on any `.no_activate(true)` in fork code; S-30's spotlight panel, merged meanwhile, had the same flag and lost it too, so "Try it now" no longer deactivates the Hub as a side effect). (2) **No AX call into our own process.** With the note in front, S-05's capture/read, S-04's paste verification, S-21's menu Copy and S-34's watcher would query this process from the AX thread, the call that crashed an unbundled build in S-20 (Finding 2). `fork::ax::is_own_pid` now guards all of them: our window gets the verdict `unknown (own_app)` (paste, fail-open), `focus_moved` still compares pids, verification is `unverified` (test `our_own_window_pastes_fail_open_and_still_detects_focus_moved`). (3) **⌥S focuses the note** on the first open too: both open paths end in `present` (`unminimize`, `show`, `set_focus`, which makes the window key and activates the accessory app); before, a newly built window was never focused (test `every_open_path_focuses_the_note`). Builder check (2026-11-03, `--no-bundle` debug build, portable `com.wisprbro.fixbcheck`, no speakers, under `gui.lock`): ⌥S with a TextEdit document in front made the test app frontmost and typed keys landed in the note (notes row 1 `Zz`, TextEdit unchanged); a take started with SIGUSR2 from the note kept the app frontmost at +0.2, +1.0 and +3.0 s (`context: read own_app`, `pill: web view built`), and the app stayed up after the stop. Not checked: the take's text arriving in the note (no speech model was loaded and no WAV path exists), the bundled identity. The full S-24.1/.2 rerun is queued for the Mac wave (MAC-TEST-SCRIPT S-24).

### S-25 Insights

- **Goal:** Wispr's Insights, computed locally.
- **User-visible outcome:** Insights with tabs Usage / Voice. Usage: words per minute (speech time) compared with the user's typing speed (from the typing test, else "average typist: 40 wpm"), fixes made (dictionary fixes, AI words corrected), total words, usage by `usage_category` (documents, AI prompts, email, work messages, personal messages, other), a streak heatmap (bands 0 / 1-249 / 250-499 / 500-749 / 750+), and "Save as image". Voice: locked until 1,700 words with blurred cards, then cards for a frequent phrase, peak time and app, most used word, most corrected word.
- **R2 rows closed:** C-50, C-51, C-52, C-54, C-55, C-56, C-57
- **R4 parts:** Recharts 3.10.1 (MIT, adopt, lazy-loaded); react-activity-calendar 3.2.1 (MIT, adopt); html-to-image (MIT, adopt); stopwords-iso (MIT, data); similar (fix counts, S-12a).
- **New fork files:** `fork/stats/{insights,voice}.rs`, `src/fork/insights/{InsightsPage,UsageTab,VoiceTab,Heatmap,CategoryChart}.tsx`.
- **Tests:** aggregates from `daily_stats`/`corrections`; locked/unlocked states; image export.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** "Save as image" works in the release build under the CSP (§4.11).
- **Exit criteria:** numbers match a hand-computed fixture; no population percentile anywhere.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-09, S-11, S-12a, S-13.
- **As built (2026-10-31), decisions that refine this block** (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Insights (S-25)"; evidence in `docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-S-25.md`):
  - **Files:** as planned, plus `fork/db/insights.rs` (the read queries; `fork/stats/` stays pure), `fork/commands/insights.rs` (`fork_insights_usage`, `fork_insights_voice`, `fork_insights_save_image`), `fork/stats/tables/stopwords-iso.json` (stopwords-iso 1.1.0, unmodified, MIT; NOTICE, PORTS.md, `LICENSES/MIT-stopwords-iso.txt`), `src/fork/insights/{useInsights,saveImage,format,heatColors}.ts` and `insights.css` (heat and bar tokens). No Handy file, no migration, no new event (S-09's `ForkStatsUpdated` drives the live refresh). `UsageCategory` gained `ALL`, `parse` and a specta type.
  - **Usage tab:** speaking speed (S-09's WPM on speech time) × the typing test's `typing_wpm` ("× your typing speed") or 40 wpm ("× the speed of an average typist") until S-29b exists; total words and dictations; fixes = `fixes_dictionary` + `fixes_llm` (S-12a counts runs of changed words, so the label is "AI cleanup fixes", not "words corrected"); "Where you dictate" = all six `usage_category` values in a fixed order with their share of the words; streak card = current and longest streak, active days in the last year and a 365-day heatmap with the planned bands (0 / 1-249 / 250-499 / 500-749 / 750+). No percentile anywhere (C-51), tested.
  - **Voice tab:** locked below 1,700 lifetime words (the Home bar's count, DEC-50) with a progress bar and four blurred cards whose text is generic sample text (`aria-hidden`), never the user's data. Unlocked: **your phrase** (3-5 words inside a clause, counted once per dictation, at least 3 dictations, score = dictations × words, never only fillers), **peak time and app** (local hour of day and app with the most words; the app stands in for Wispr's "place", R2 C-57), **most used word** (stopwords-iso for the history's languages plus English, S-12a's hesitations and fillers, numbers and one-letter words left out), **most corrected word**. The cards read the newest 10,000 counted dictations still in History (text retention can remove older ones; the totals stay in `daily_stats`); 10,000 dictations take 1.4 s in a debug build, off the database lock.
  - **Corrections (S-12a left this to S-25):** no `corrections` rows for LLM edits. "Most corrected word" diffs `asr_text` (verbatim) against `final_text` word by word and counts substitutions of 1-3 words per side, so rules, Handy's word fixes and AI cleanup all count and a removed filler does not; `corrections` rows of source `flag`/`learn` (S-15b, S-34) are added since they do not change the text; `rule` rows are already in the diff.
  - **Parts:** Recharts 3.10.1 (horizontal bars, lazy chunk), react-activity-calendar 3.2.1 (lazy chunk, `Intl` month/weekday names, locale week start), html-to-image 1.11.13 (loaded on click; one warm-up render for WebKit fonts), all MIT. **similar not adopted** (as in S-12a: the in-tree LCS does it). Recharts brings `es-toolkit` (MIT, ships a Lodash NOTICE; listed in NOTICE §5) and Redux error-text hosts (`redux.js.org`, `redux-toolkit.js.org`, added to `ci/inert-hosts.txt`). Budgets: `package.json` 42, `Cargo.toml` 20 (`base64`, already in the tree).
  - **Save as image:** save panel first (default name "<app> insights <date>.png"), then a 2× PNG of the dated line plus the visible tab (buttons and tabs left out), written by `fork_insights_save_image` (`.png` name, PNG signature and ≤ 25 MB checked). No sharing (R2 C-56).
  - **Accessibility and RTL:** the bar chart is `aria-hidden` with an `sr-only` list of the same numbers; the heatmap is `role="img"` with a summary and a text legend; figures are `<dl>`/text. In RTL the bars grow from the right (reversed axis, labels on the right); the heatmap keeps time left to right. Playwright: axe on both tabs (locked and unlocked) in light and dark, Arabic RTL, keyboard-only.
  - **Translations:** 69 `insights.*` keys machine-translated with the `llama-server` backend (Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507), the worst literal results corrected by hand (de, nl, ja, ar), all flagged `machine` for S-37. Counts use `{{count, number}}`. Known gap for S-37: languages with more plural forms than one/other (Arabic "many", Polish "few") fall back to English for those counts, as everywhere in `fork.json`.
  - **Real-Mac result (2026-10-31, M5 Max, macOS 26.6.2; `docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-S-25.md`):** release bundle (the real CSP), test identity `com.wisprbro.s25check`, portable profile with seeded synthetic history (`scripts/wb-s25/seed.py`, no audio). Usage tab equal to numbers computed in Python from the tables (150 wpm, 3.8×, 1,075 words / 116 dictations, fixes 6 / 13, shares 8/9/20/52/10/0 %, streak 4 / 8, 15 active days); Voice locked at 1,075 words with blurred cards, unlocked after crossing 1,700 with the expected phrase, peak hour and app, most used and most corrected word; "Save as image" through the system save panel wrote 2× PNGs of both tabs with the page's own fonts, chart and heatmap (S-25.1). The export's 24 px margin was added after that run (Playwright-checked; no new resource type under the CSP).
  - **Not verified:** the release build's Web Inspector console, VoiceOver by ear, a live unlock by a real take (seeded across a relaunch; the live refresh is covered in Playwright), other locales in the real app, the Developer ID build (F-02).

### S-26a App menus and tray

- **Goal:** Wispr's menu structure, local and honest.
- **User-visible outcome:** app menus Dictation ("N words dictated", Copy last transcript with preview, Microphone ▸, Languages ▸), Vocabulary ("N dictionary words", "N snippets", Add word…, New snippet…), Help (registered with `set_as_help_menu_for_nsapp()`, so macOS adds its search box; Documentation, Shortcuts, Microphone, Languages) while Edit/Window keep ⌘C/⌘V/⌘Z working. Counts update live. Tray: Open Wispr Bro, Check for updates… (Handy's existing check until S-28 routes it to `fork/update`), Paste last transcript (disabled until one exists), Shortcuts, Microphone ▸, Languages ▸, Documentation, Quit (no Model ▸ or Unload). All menu strings come from `fork/i18n.rs`.
- **R2 rows closed:** A-56, A-57, A-59, A-63, C-100, C-101
- **Regression protected (HAVE):** A-55, A-62, A-69
- **R4 parts:** Tauri 2.11.5 `Menu::default` + inserted submenus and `Submenu::set_as_help_menu_for_nsapp` (MIT/Apache, W-11).
- **Handy files touched:** `tray.rs` (`fork::tray::extend`, already present from F-01).
- **New fork files:** `fork/app_menu.rs` (extensible builder: later slices add items by id), `fork/tray.rs` (items).
- **Tests:** menu structure snapshot (Edit present, Help registered); disabled states; counts live (C-213 menu part, C-105); localized strings in 3 locales.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** ⌘C/⌘V/⌘Z in Hub text fields; the Help search box finds menu items; the tray icon comes back after the menu bar process (ControlCenter) restarts (A-69).
- **Exit criteria:** R1 §6 and §11 menu items present (minus out-of-scope ones and the S-26b items).
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-13, S-14, S-06, S-15a, S-16, S-17.
- **As built (2026-11-02):** `fork/app_menu.rs` (+ `app_menu/languages.json`, `app_menu/tests.rs`) builds both menus from one cached snapshot read on a worker (never the main thread) and pure `MenuSpec` trees, so later slices add items by id. App menu: Tauri's `Menu::default` (Edit keeps ⌘Z/⌘X/⌘C/⌘V/⌘A, Window ⌘M) with every label localized from `rust.menu.*`, Dictation and Vocabulary after Edit, our items in Tauri's Help submenu registered with `set_as_help_menu_for_nsapp()`. Tray: the existing F-01 `tray.rs` hook now replaces Handy's items (S-07b's model-item removal folded in); Handy's ids (`settings`, `check_updates`, `cancel`, `quit`, `secure_input_warning`) are kept so Handy's handler runs them. Counts, preview and choices update in place on fork/Handy change events; a submenu is rebuilt only when its shape changes. No new Handy hook, no migration, no new command; one new event (`fork-menu-action`) drives Hub deep links. **Decisions:** Languages ▸ lists the user's languages (a click removes one, never the last) plus "Add or remove languages…" to Settings, since adding from a menu could start a model download and ~100 entries belong in the searchable picker; Copy last transcript shows a one-line ≤ 40-character preview and "(text hidden)" for password-field takes; Help › Documentation opens `docs/fork/user` on GitHub; Help/tray Shortcuts open the S-14 hotkeys panel; Help › Microphone/Languages scroll Settings › General; Vocabulary › Add word…/New snippet… open the pages' dialogs; Check for updates… is greyed when update checks are off and dropped when forced off (as Handy). The 46 new strings are copy-English/`untranslated` in 25 locales pending the wave batch. Gates after rebasing on F-02: 1,394 Rust tests passed/32 ignored, 329 Playwright passed/10 skipped (installed Chrome; incl. `menus.spec.ts` with axe light/dark, RTL and keyboard-only), fork clippy 0, guards, ESLint, tsc, translation parity and formatting. MockRuntime has no native menus (`ForkRuntime::native_menus`), so tests never build AppKit menus or list real microphones. **Not run (queued for the UI Mac wave, `scripts/wb-s26a/`, MAC-TEST-SCRIPT S-26a.0-.4):** ⌘C/⌘V/⌘Z in Hub fields, the Help search box, tray recovery after a ControlCenter restart, live native counts, the tray by eye. **Mac wave 1 (2026-11-02):** S-26a.0-.4 and the tray by AX PASS (`docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-macwave-1.md`).

Share with a friend: copy the download link or `brew install --cask` line, macOS share sheet; no tracking. About: version, GPL-3.0, source link, credit to Handy.
