No Handy source file (only its two issue templates, below), migration or dependency; every link is on github.com and opens only on a click (`[link]` stays empty). **Decisions:** the popover keeps Wispr's three groups as Updates (What's new → the GitHub releases page; Handy's bundled notes are Handy's), Essentials (Shortcuts, Microphone, Languages, S-31 lessons through `setHelpLessons`, Documentation) and Get in touch (Report a problem…, Send feedback); S-13's Models and History entries left the popover (both stay reachable from Settings and Dictation). ⌘/ from the menu opens the issue at once (the acceptance check); the Hub's Report a problem shows a dialog first with Copy diagnostics (S-27's sanitized text, never in the URL) and Open GitHub; the issue body points there. Send feedback is an issue labelled `feedback` (Discussions may be off on a new repo). Issue text is English whatever the app language (maintainers triage in English). Share: `releases/latest` (no query string, the same for everyone); the `brew install --cask fjenn/tap/wispr-bro` line is built but hidden (`BREW_CASK_PUBLISHED = false` in `src/fork/hub/links.ts`) until a tap exists; Copy uses the web clipboard API; the share sheet gets the localized `rust.share.message` and the link as an `NSURL`. About keeps S-28's update panel and adds GPL-3.0 with a licence link, credit to Handy by CJ Pais (MIT) with a link, and Support development (no paid plan: Report a problem / Send feedback). **Issue templates:** Handy's `.github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/` (blank issues off, links to Handy's Discussions) is replaced by `wb-bug-report.md` and `wb-feedback.md` with blank issues on, so the prefilled URL opens as a form; the two Handy files are budgeted in `ci/hooks.txt` and `.github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/wb-*` joined the guards' fork paths. The 40 new strings (5 removed with the old Help items and Share placeholder) are copy-English/`untranslated` in 25 locales pending the wave batch. Gates after rebasing on `wispr-bro` eaa7f126 (wave A, S-38 part 1, S-34, S-28 and S-29b merged): 1,523 Rust tests passed/32 ignored, 511 Playwright passed/10 skipped (installed Chrome; incl. `help-share.spec.ts` with axe light/dark × LTR/RTL, reduced motion and keyboard-only), fork clippy 0, guards, ESLint, tsc, translation parity and formatting. One existing load flake was fixed in the same wave: S-36's spec clicks the saved-value toggles instead of `check()` (S-25's voice-profile speed gate, which also failed under load, was already moved to CPU time by S-38 part 1). **Not run (queued for the UI Mac wave, `scripts/wb-s26b/`, MAC-TEST-SCRIPT S-26b.0-.2):** ⌘/ opening the browser on the real menu, the share sheet on screen, the tray items by eye.

### S-27 Data and Privacy, export, delete all, diagnostics

- **Goal:** the privacy promise made visible and controllable.
- **User-visible outcome:** Data and Privacy page in plain words: everything stays on this Mac; the two network uses with hosts; no account, telemetry or cloud; where data lives (data, models, recordings, logs, each with "Show in Finder"); retention (text: forever / 1 year / 90 / 30 days; audio: 14 days default, never keep / 1 / 7 / 30 / 90 days / forever); context awareness toggle; update checks toggle (bound to the fork setting from F-07). S-34 adds its auto-learn row here. "Export my data" (zip) and "Delete all my data" (typed confirmation; optional "also delete downloaded models"). Crash reports and Copy/Save diagnostics.
- **R2 rows closed:** B-205, E-64, E-66, E-67, E-68, E-79
- **Regression protected (HAVE):** E-65, E-88
- **R4 parts:** csv 1.4, zip 4.x (in tree, adopt direct); Voquill log sanitizer (from F-08); TypeWhisper `UserDataEraser` / MacParakeet eraser checklist (GPL, reference); `std::panic::set_hook` + tauri-plugin-log (in tree, W-24).
- **Handy files touched:** none beyond F-08 hooks.
- **New fork files:** `fork/export.rs`, `fork/erase.rs`, `fork/diagnostics/{crash,bundle}.rs` (crash file, `.ips` pickup, sanitized zip), `src/fork/settings/DataPrivacy.tsx`.
- **Erase order:** stop recording and jobs → unload models and stop the sidecar → delete `history.db`, `fork.db` (+ `-wal`, `-shm`), recordings, settings stores, logs, (models if chosen) → relaunch into onboarding.
- **Tests:** export contents and schema version; erase leaves no files in the app data dir except models when kept; portable mode paths (E-88); crash file creation and inbox entry.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** export opens in Finder; erase then relaunch shows onboarding; `brew uninstall --zap` paths match (S-39).
- **Exit criteria:** every statement on the page is true and checked by a test or the egress check.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-09, S-14, F-08.
- **As built (2026-11-02):** `fork/export.rs`, `fork/erase.rs` (+ `erase_tests.rs`), `fork/diagnostics/bundle.rs`, `.ips` pickup in `fork/diagnostics/crash.rs`, `fork/commands/privacy.rs` (six fork commands) and the real `src/fork/settings/DataPrivacy.tsx` page replace S-14's placeholder; details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Data and Privacy (S-27)". No Handy file, no migration, no new setting (retention, context awareness and update checks bind the F-07 settings); one dependency, `zip` 4 (already in the tree; `Cargo.toml` budget 27). Decisions: **export** = `manifest.json` (`schema_version` 1), `history.csv` + `history.json` read through `fork::db::handy` (R5 §4.3: no SQL on Handy's `history.db`, so no raw copy of it), a `VACUUM INTO` snapshot of `fork.db`, both settings stores and the recordings (streamed); models and logs excluded; atomic temp-file rename; revealed in Finder. **Delete all** = exact typed word `DELETE` (untranslated, checked again in Rust), ready product/test identity only; stop recording/jobs/downloads → unload the speech model and stop the sidecar → close the settings stores and clear WebKit storage → delete the data and log dirs (symlinks unlinked, unsafe roots refused; with "keep models" `models/`, `huggingface/`, `llm/verified/` survive) → relaunch, where fresh fork settings start onboarding; a failed phase stops the list and never relaunches. **Diagnostics** = sanitized tails of `.log`/`.txt`/`.ips` only (Copy: 4 files × 48 KB to the pasteboard; Save: zip with `diagnostics.txt` + `logs/`), never databases, settings, History or audio. **Crash reports**: our own recent macOS `.ips` (bundle id in the JSON header, < 7 days) are copied sanitized into the log dir as crash files, so the existing next-launch inbox notice and diagnostics include them. Exit criterion: `privacy::tests::page_statements_match_the_code` pins the English page text (two hosts, retention defaults, update default, export/delete wording) to the code, with the export/erase/bundle tests and the egress guard covering the rest. Scripted Mac checks `scripts/wb-s27/{seed,verify}.py`, `zap-paths.sh` with S-25's `ui.swift` (MAC-TEST-SCRIPT S-27.1-S-27.5). **Mac wave 1 (2026-11-02):** S-27.1-.4 PASS (zap paths recorded for S-39); S-27.5 FAIL: `.ips` pickup redacts by pattern only, so a body line without a `Transcript:` prefix keeps its text (`docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-macwave-1.md`). Gates after the rebase onto F-02: 1,399 Rust tests/32 ignored and 331 Playwright/10 skipped passed (three axe sweeps timed out in the full run at load ~60 from parallel builders and passed when re-run alone; before the rebase the full suite passed in one run) (new `privacy.spec.ts`: hosts, folders, retention persistence, export/diagnostics paths, typed confirmation, failure states, keyboard-only, RTL, axe light/dark with reduced motion), plus fork clippy 0, fmt, ESLint, tsc, both translation checks, guards and `wb:format:check`; two load-flaky specs (pill dark mode, Style Cleanup radio) were fixed, not re-run. The new strings are copy-English/`untranslated` pending the wave's machine batch. **Not run (queued for the Mac wave):** export revealed in Finder, a real delete-all and relaunch into onboarding, the zap path list (S-39 compares it), Copy/Save diagnostics on the real pasteboard and save panel, a real `.ips` pickup and inbox notice. **Not verified:** VoiceOver by ear (S-37), the native save panel's `.zip` handling beyond the page's suffix fix, delete-all while a long take or download is actually running. **Mac wave 1 fixes (`fix/wave1-a`, 2026-11-03):** (a) S-27.5: the `.ips` copy is rebuilt from an allow-list (`fork/diagnostics/ips.rs`: header versions/ids, exception, termination code, thread frames, identifier-like thread/queue names, used images' names/uuids/addresses) before the sanitizer; `asi`, `vmSummary`, paths, user/device ids and any non-JSON body are dropped; tested with a realistic macOS 26 report carrying the marker in every free-text field. (b) S-27.2: Delete all writes S-28's relaunch marker with `show`, which clears a `--start-hidden` the erased process had for that one start (other flags kept; not counted as an update relaunch). Both re-checks are queued for the next Mac wave.
- **Mac wave 2 fix (2026-11-03, `fix/wave2`):** after a crash the next launch stopped in AppKit's "reopen windows?" alert (no bar, no hotkeys). `fork/restore.rs` opts out of window restoration from `early_init` (registration-domain `ApplePersistenceIgnoreState` = YES, `NSQuitAlwaysKeepsWindows` = NO) and marks the Hub window non-restorable; tests check both and that `early_init` keeps the call. Queued: MAC-TEST-SCRIPT S-27.6.

### S-28 Updates that never interrupt

- **Goal:** Wispr's update policy with our signed feed.
- **User-visible outcome:** updates download in the background and install only when idle (no dictation or processing, ≥ 20 min since the last take) or at quit; the app relaunches quietly to the bar; after 3 failed installs it pauses 24 h. The Hub footer (S-13) shows "Up to date", "Update ready · Restart now" or "Couldn't check · Retry" next to the version. Tray "Check for updates…" now uses `fork/update` and is honest when checks are off. What's New appears once per version, never while recording. Beta channel opt-in in Advanced (endpoint selection from F-02).
- **R2 rows closed:** C-99, E-51, E-53, E-75, E-215
- **Regression protected (HAVE):** A-60
- **R4 parts:** tauri-plugin-updater 2.10.1 (keep; `download()` then `install()`); MacParakeet `SparkleUpdateGuard.swift` cases (GPL, port as tests); Talkify deferral (MIT, idea); Handy offline What's New (keep content mechanism).
- **Handy files touched:** none (Handy's `UpdateChecker.tsx` is not mounted by the fork footer).
- **New fork files:** `fork/update/{scheduler,idle_gate,failures}.rs`, `src/fork/hub/UpdateStatus.tsx`, `src/fork/whats-new/*.md` (our content).
- **Tests:** guard cases (blocks during recording, vetoes relaunch mid-take, skips dev builds, 3-failure pause); channel URL selection; tray item routed to the fork check.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** RS-12 on the real slice; update during a long hands-free take waits.
- **Exit criteria:** never installs mid-take in a 2-day beta test.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / F-02, S-14, S-18, S-26a.
- **As built (2026-11-02):** `fork/update/{scheduler,idle_gate,failures,relaunch}.rs` on F-02's `check`/`download_verified`; `src/fork/hub/UpdateStatus.tsx` (footer `UpdateStatus`, About `UpdatePanel`), `src/fork/whats-new/` (`WhatsNewGate.tsx`, `releaseNotes.ts`, `1.0.0-alpha.1.md`); details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Updates that never interrupt (S-28)". No migration, no new setting; one Handy hook: `main.rs` passes `CliArgs::parse()` through `fork::cli_args` (budget 1 → 3). Decisions: (1) **Schedule:** first check 60 s after launch, then every 6 h (E-53, on by default E-75), a failed check retries after 30 min; a newer release downloads and is signature-checked in the background and is held in memory; the update-ready notice (S-18) is its producer. (2) **Idle gate** (E-215): no install while a take records, transcribes, cleans up or is delivered or a transform runs (`ForkRuntime::update_busy`), not within 20 min of the last take (launch counts as one), and not while one of our windows has focus (the user is in the app: the footer offers Restart now). An installed update relaunches only when no take runs (the veto). MacParakeet's `SparkleUpdateGuard` cases are ported as behaviour + tests (no code; PORTS/NOTICE). (3) **Dev builds** never check on their own (debug builds unless the loopback test feed is set; versions `0.0.0`/`dev`; plain Handy identity); a manual check and Restart now still work. (4) **Quiet relaunch:** a marker in the log dir before `request_restart()`; the next start reads it and sets `start_hidden` when the Hub was hidden, so the app comes back to the bar without a window or focus change. (5) **Failures:** a bad signature or failed install counts; 3 in a row pause background updates 24 h (`update-state.json`, cleared once that version landed); manual Retry overrides. (6) **Found and handled:** tauri-plugin-updater asks for an administrator password (AppleScript) when it cannot move the bundle; background and quit installs are skipped when the bundle's folder is not writable (only Restart now installs; About says why). (7) **Checks off** drops a downloaded update; the tray reads "Update checks are off…" (opens Data and Privacy) instead of a greyed item; Advanced's toggle now binds the fork setting (Handy's toggle there wrote only Handy's value, which the next start overwrote). Tray "Check for updates…" runs the fork check; with an update ready it reads "Restart to update to X". A channel change drops a ready release of the other channel. (8) **What's New:** our notes only (Handy's `src/content/release-notes/` never show), semver with prerelease so each alpha has its own note, once per version (`whats_new_last_seen_version`), never while `busy`; fresh installs see none. **Tests:** Rust 32 new (machine: schedule, retry, dev builds, quiet window, focus, busy, relaunch veto, Restart now, three-failure pause and expiry, bad signature, checks off, channel change, quit install, late results, password-needing bundle; executor on the loopback stand with the real plugin download/verify and a fake install/relaunch: waits mid-take and in the quiet window, Restart now refused mid-take, installs and relaunches hidden, quit install without relaunch, failure persisted; failures file; relaunch marker; tray specs and click routing); Playwright `tests/fork/updates.spec.ts` (every footer state, Check/Retry/Restart now/Turn on, refusal mid-take, live events, About panel incl. pause/dev/password notes, What's New once/never while dictating/fresh install, keyboard-only, RTL, axe light/dark with reduced motion). Gates after the rebase onto S-38 part 1: 1,483 Rust tests passed/32 ignored, 460 Playwright passed/10 skipped, fork clippy 0, fmt, ESLint, tsc, both translation checks, guards and `wb:format:check`. S-38's Hub parking needs nothing here: checks, installs and the relaunch run in Rust; the footer and About read the state when the Hub is rebuilt, and a parked Hub counts as hidden for the quiet relaunch. **Not verified (queued for the Mac wave, MAC-TEST-SCRIPT S-28.0-.5):** `scripts/wb-f02/update-e2e.sh` S-28.1-.4 (real background download, a real hands-free take deferring the install, hidden relaunch with no window, install at quit, three-failure pause, no password prompt), footer and tray by eye. **Not verified (release wave):** RS-12 on real GitHub releases and the exit criterion (no install mid-take in a 2-day beta test), S-28.6. 31 new strings are copy-English/`untranslated` in 25 locales pending the wave's machine batch.
- **Mac wave 2 fix (2026-11-03, `fix/wave2`):** S-28.1d-f relaunched shown although System Events had hidden the app: `hub_hidden` asked only Tauri's `is_visible`, and a hidden app's windows still report visible. The decision is now `relaunch_hidden(HubView)`: hidden when the app is hidden (`NSApp.isHidden`), the Hub is parked (S-38), missing, ordered out, minimised or not on screen (occlusion state); AppKit is read on the main thread through `ForkRuntime::update_appkit_view`. Unit test over the decision and a mock-runtime test over the sampling; the flag's path to the new process (marker → `--start-hidden`) is unchanged and tested. `update-e2e.sh` now seeds its throwaway profile with every shortcut unbound and a cloned model cache, and its take plays a silent WAV through the test microphone. Queued: MAC-TEST-SCRIPT S-28.7.
- **Mac wave 3 fixes (2026-11-03, `fix/wave3`):** finding 1 (S-28.1d-f): a take unhid the hidden app because the bar's panels could hide with it and AppKit unhides a hidden app when such a window is ordered in, `orderFrontRegardless` included (confirmed with a throwaway AppKit program). The resting bar, the `fork-pill` web view and the spotlight now have `canHide` off (`fork/pill/native.rs` `stay_when_app_hidden`): they stay on screen when the app is hidden and the app stays hidden. Decision: the bar staying visible after ⌘H is the intended behaviour (it is the dictation entry point). Guard test `bar_panels_never_unhide_a_hidden_app`; `update-e2e.sh` adds S-28.1h/.1i. Finding 4: What's New now waits for the first-use chooser, lessons, the spotlight and any other dialog (plus 400 ms, or the closing dialog's focus hand-back dismissed it), and a new user (the chooser still due) gets the running version marked seen without a note. Decision: "new user" = the chooser would open by itself, since the fresh-store default only covers a store created by Handy itself. Playwright: two new What's New specs. **Queued:** S-28.8, S-28.9.
- **Mac wave 4 fixes (2026-11-03, `fix/wave4`):** finding 1 (S-28.8, 1h/1i): the first take after the per-take web view was destroyed still unhid the hidden app. Cause (reproduced off screen, `scripts/wb-f02/unhide-repro.swift`): wry 0.55 calls `NSApplication::activate()` after every top-level web view build; on a hidden app that does not activate it but unhides it ~0.5 s later. The window itself was already built `visible(false)` and is never ordered in during the build, so canHide was not the gap. The `fork-pill` and `fork-spotlight` builds now run inside `fork/pill/activation.rs` `without_app_activation`, which swaps `activate` and `activateIgnoringOtherApps:` for no-ops for the synchronous build and restores them (also on unwind). Decision: a guard around our two builds rather than patching wry (thin layer) or keeping the web view alive while hidden (S-38's idle memory; a take after a hidden relaunch would still build it). The Hub and Scratchpad builds keep wry's activation. Guard tests: `activation.rs` (2) and the extended `bar_panels_never_unhide_a_hidden_app`. Finding 2 (S-28.9 edge): What's New now blocks on the take itself (`useTakeRunning`: `recording-state-event` plus Handy's `is_recording` at mount), not only on `update.busy` sampled every 10 s. Playwright: a take started 2 s before the Hub opened (no note until it stops) and a take starting while the note is open (hidden at once, not marked seen). No new strings. **Queued:** S-28.8, S-28.9 re-checks.

### Onboarding

### S-29a Onboarding: welcome, permissions and the two tests

- **Goal:** Wispr's onboarding start without an account: tiny tests with immediate yes/no, never blocked by downloads.
- **User-visible outcome:** a stepper Welcome › Permissions › Setup › Practice (R1's PERSONALIZE stage is a referral screen and is out, §2.3) with a Back link and a Help chip. Welcome (privacy promise; the starter download starts here) → Move to Applications if needed → Permissions (Microphone, Accessibility) with a picture of each macOS prompt and pane, rows that tick when granted → Test your microphone (copy: wired or built-in mics work best, Bluetooth can drop out; live bars; "Do the bars move when you talk?"; Change microphone opens the list in the S-08 order) → Test your shortcut (an on-screen fn/🌐 key lights while pressed; Edit shortcut opens the hotkeys panel; "No" leads to a diagnosis: tap not live, Secure Input, no fn key → switch to Ctrl+Opt, Globe bound to emoji/input source → the DEC-44 path).
- **R2 rows closed:** B-01, B-02, B-03, B-10, B-21, B-22, B-201, E-216
- **Regression protected (HAVE):** B-04, B-05, B-09, B-11, B-12, B-13, B-46, B-212
- **R4 parts:** Handy onboarding hooks `usePermissions.ts`, `useTryEngine.ts`, `Demo.tsx` (MIT, reuse by import); MacParakeet `OnboardingViewModelTests` (GPL-3.0, port as behavioural spec); hex `native/permission_guide.m` and OpenWhispr `PermissionGuideOverlay.tsx` (MIT, port); Yap "reset and re-grant" (MIT, idea).
- **Handy files touched:** ⚠ `src/App.tsx` (fork onboarding replaces the switch; Handy onboarding files untouched).
- **New fork files:** `src/fork/onboarding/{OnboardingFlow,Stepper,Welcome,MoveToApplications,Permissions,MicTest,ShortcutTest}.tsx`, `src/fork/onboarding/art/*.svg` (macOS prompt mock-ups per version, light/dark), `fork/onboarding/state.rs` (logic over the onboarding state stored in `fork/settings.rs`, RE-16).
- **Tests:** Playwright for every step and branch incl. each "No" diagnosis; resume after relaunch at each step; preview mode; never waits on downloads.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** clean Mac: permissions to shortcut test in ≤ 2 min; each diagnosis branch reachable.
- **Exit criteria:** every step and branch covered; no step waits on a download.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-01, S-03, S-07a, S-08, S-14.
- **As built (2026-11-02), decisions that refine this block** (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Onboarding (S-29a)"; procedure in `docs/fork/MAC-TEST-SCRIPT.md` S-29a, queued for the Mac wave): (1) **Steps** (`fork/onboarding/state.rs` ↔ `src/fork/onboarding/steps.ts`, one test keeps them equal): `welcome`, `move_to_applications` (only outside an Applications folder), `languages` (stage Welcome), `permissions`, `mic_test`, `shortcut_test` (stage Setup); Practice is shown in the stepper and filled by S-29b, so until then "Finish setup" on the shortcut test completes onboarding (fork state + Handy's `onboarding_completed`). Each step is saved before the screen changes, so a relaunch resumes at it; Back drops later completion. (2) **Languages pulled forward from S-29b** (owner brief): S-12a measured nl/fr filler removal 95 % → 67 % without spoken languages, so the first run asks. Preselected from macOS (Handy's `suggestedLanguages`), Handy's `LanguagePicker` reused by import, saves `spoken_languages` (and the Chinese script when Chinese is newly picked) and calls `fork_speech_setup_refresh` (S-07b re-picks). The note states the picker's language count and names weak languages; S-29b keeps its practice/celebration/typing-test scope. (3) **Handy's flow is gone, not hooked:** `App.tsx` imports the fork flow (1 changed line, budget 46 → 48); S-07a/b's `OnboardingFlow.tsx` hook and `firstRun.{ts,css}` were removed (that file is upstream again), which also retires S-07a's "Try it / All set names a model" gap: no onboarding screen names a model (Playwright checks every screen). (4) **Never waits on the download:** no screen reads speech-setup state (asserted); the footer says setup continues in the background. (5) **Permissions:** Handy's `usePermissions` (MIT, reuse by import) for request, polling and the Settings fallback; pictures of the prompt and the System Settings pane per row are our own abstract SVGs (no Apple artwork or text), one set for macOS 13-15 and one for macOS 26, inlined so they follow the app theme. Hex's `permission_guide.m` / OpenWhispr's overlay were not ported: in-window pictures cover the need without a floating panel; Yap's reset-and-regrant was not needed in the flow. (6) **Mic test:** S-08's meter starts on its own (stopped on leaving), copy recommends wired/built-in and warns about Bluetooth, "No" or "Change microphone" opens S-08's list (its order). (7) **Shortcut test:** a backend test mode (`fork_onboarding_key_test`, self-expiring after 2 min, renewed every 60 s, off on unmount) makes trigger presses light the key and start no take; it also starts the tap if it could not start at launch (Accessibility granted during onboarding works without a relaunch, `shortcut::retry_tap`). The test can pass by the key lighting or by the user's Yes. "Edit shortcut" opens the S-14 panel in a dialog (the floating onboarding shortcut button S-14 added was removed). (8) **Diagnoses:** "No" opens the likeliest cause, the others one click away: tap not live (engine not running) → Accessibility pane + Try again; Secure Input (Handy's live check) → explanation and live state; Globe on emoji/input source/dictation → DEC-44 Do Nothing on the button only (OpenWhispr `GlobeSystemAction` port, `shortcut/globe.rs`: original value saved first, restored at launch or on any binding change once fn is not the trigger, unless the user changed it since) or Keyboard settings; no fn key → the Ctrl+Option profile. System Settings panes open from Rust (`fork_onboarding_open_pane`, fixed `x-apple.systempreferences:` URLs). (9) **Move to Applications** (LetsMove's idea, no code): only on the button; `rename`, else `ditto` copy (other volume, disk image, translocation; quarantine flag cleared on the copy), `~/Applications` when `/Applications` is not writable, never overwrites; reopens the new copy and quits; "Not now" skips; a resumed run already in Applications goes on by itself. (10) **Preview** (debug) is inert: no prompts, monitoring, moves, setting writes or step saves (asserted). **Tests:** Rust (state/resume/back/complete, step parity with the UI, Globe apply/restore/crash/consent cases, key-test trigger matching and expiry, move path decisions incl. translocation and EXDEV/EROFS/EACCES, pane URLs); Playwright `tests/fork/onboarding.spec.ts` (full run, resume at every step, Move incl. failure and skip, Back, permissions granting, macOS 26 art, mic list, Edit shortcut, all four diagnoses and cross-links, preview, Help, keyboard-only, RTL, axe for five screens in light and dark with reduced motion). **Mac wave 1 (2026-11-02):** resume PASS; the move renamed the bundle into `/Applications` but the relaunched debug build panicked in Handy's debug-only bindings export (cwd `/`), so rerun the move on a release build; keytest, TCC and Globe cases need the owner (`docs/fork/test-runs/1.0.0-alpha.1-M-26-macwave-1.md`). **Not verified:** everything on a real Mac (queued: `scripts/wb-s29a/run.sh` + MAC-TEST-SCRIPT S-29a.1-.6), notably the 2-minute clean-account timing, the tap starting after a live grant, `TISUpdateFnUsageType` on this macOS and its restore, a real move from a disk image, the pictures against real macOS 26/15 panes, VoiceOver by ear (S-37); 100 new strings are copy-English/`untranslated` in 25 locales pending the wave's machine batch.

### S-29b Onboarding: languages, practice, celebration, typing test and summary

- **Goal:** the first dictation inside onboarding, celebrated, and a number for the time it can save.
- **User-visible outcome:** Languages (preselected from macOS; copy states an honest language count) → "Practice: dictate an email without holding the key" in a simulated composer (Skip always offered). If the starter is not ready, the practice still records and shows "Setting up… N%"; the composer fills when the starter arrives, and after 90 s the step moves on by itself with the take kept in History → Practice stage, dark theme: "That's it. You dictated your first text." (confetti, reduced-motion aware) → time saved: "Dictating could save you about 0 hours a week" (the counter starts at 0) → "How fast do you type?" 30-second typing test (language picker, sample paragraph per language, live wpm, seconds left, Try again, "Recalculate"), after which the counter updates, with a typing-time-per-day slider (default 2 h, DEC-50) and a "Take the typing test" link if it was skipped → launch-at-login choice and a summary of choices. The resting bar appears when setup ends (DEC-38).
- **R2 rows closed:** B-25, B-28, B-29, B-36, B-37, B-213
- **Regression protected (HAVE):** B-26, B-27
- **R4 parts:** canvas-confetti 1.9.4 (ISC, adopt); Handy `Demo.tsx` (MIT, extend); typing test and slider (W-18).
- **New fork files:** `src/fork/onboarding/{Languages,HandsFreePractice,FirstDictation,TimeSaved,TypingTest,Summary}.tsx`, `src/fork/onboarding/samples/<lang>.json`.
- **Tests:** Playwright: practice with a mocked slow starter download (records, shows progress, fills later, auto-advances at 90 s); wpm math; counter starts at 0 and updates after the test; slider; skip paths.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** 10 people on clean Macs (8 GB and 16 GB, one Intel if DEC-29): median ≤ 5 min, first dictation within 2 min of install (§5.3).
- **Exit criteria:** §5.3 met; 0 testers stuck, where stuck = needed outside help or spent > 15 min in onboarding.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-29a, S-12a, S-15a.
- **As built (2026-11-02), decisions that refine this block** (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Onboarding Practice (S-29b)"; procedure in `docs/fork/MAC-TEST-SCRIPT.md` S-29b, queued for the Mac wave): (1) **Languages** was built in S-29a (pulled forward; it sets Handy's `spoken_languages`, which S-12a's nl/fr cleanup needs, re-picks the model through S-07b and names none, with the honest count of the picker's languages and the weak ones named, B-213); S-29b adds the Practice stage after the shortcut test: steps `practice`, `first_dictation`, `time_saved`, `typing_test`, `summary` (`state.rs` ↔ `steps.ts`), resumable like the others. Files: `FirstDictation.tsx` (not `Languages.tsx`, which exists), `samples/{en,nl,fr,de,es,it,pt}.json` + `samples/index.ts`, `typing.ts` (+ `typing.test.ts`), `practice.css`, `fork/onboarding/practice.rs`. (2) **The practice take never pastes** (Handy's Try it pastes into its own note): `fork_onboarding_practice` turns on a 2-minute, renewed, self-expiring mode; a take that _starts with Wispr Bro frontmost_ is marked at record start (source `practice`, so DEC-11 keeps it out of the stats and paste-last), and its delivery hook emits `fork-practice-take` with the final text instead of a paste (outcome `confirmed`, clipboard untouched); a take with another app in front stays an ordinary dictation. Hooks are fork-internal only (`context::on_record_start`, `insert::deliver_as`, `models::setup::{queue_take,on_job_finished}`); no Handy file. (3) **Slow starter:** a queued practice take emits `queued`; the screen then reads `fork_speech_setup_status` once and follows `fork-speech-setup` ("Setting up… N%"), fills the composer from the queued take's History text when S-07c transcribes it (only while the screen is open), and moves on to the celebration 90 s after the take was queued; the take is in History either way. No other Practice screen reads the download. (4) **Hands-free is taught, not enforced:** copy says fn+Space (⌃⌥+Space on the fallback profile) and fn to finish; a held take also counts. Skip (top right) goes to time saved and skips the celebration; no text 20 s after a take → "Nothing came through". (5) **Celebration** dark in both themes (`.wb-stage-dark` re-points the active tokens), canvas-confetti 1.9.4 (ISC, adopted) on its own canvas without a worker (no blob: script under the CSP), none with reduced motion. Handy's `Demo.tsx` scene was not extended: the drawn composer is simpler and S-30 owns the scene engine. (6) **Numbers (W-18, DEC-50):** wpm = correctly placed characters ÷ 5 per minute since the first key (typos lower it; paste and drop blocked; the test ends at 30 s or when the paragraph is typed); time saved = hours a day × 5 workdays × max(0, 1 − typing wpm ÷ 130), rounded to the half hour; 130 wpm is a conservative everyday speaking rate, stated on screen; until measured, "Skip the test" uses the 40 wpm average (S-25's fallback) with a "Take the typing test" link; slider 0.5-8 h in half hours, default 2 h, saved to `typing_hours_per_day`; Recalculate saves `typing_wpm`. Samples are our own paragraphs for the Latin-script languages (an IME-typed test would measure the IME); the picker starts at the first spoken language with a sample. (7) **Summary:** languages, shortcut, microphone in use, typing speed (no model row, B-36/B-207); "Open Wispr Bro when you log in" is checked by default and written once (Handy's `autostart_enabled`) when "Start dictating" completes setup; a failed write never blocks finishing. Completion shows the resting bar (DEC-38, S-03). (8) The shortcut test's button now says Continue (its "Finish setup" string was removed from every locale). **Tests:** Rust `fork::onboarding::practice` (mark, front app, consume once, queued WAVs, close forgets, expiry, bound) and the updated step parity/resume tests; `bun src/fork/onboarding/typing.test.ts`; Playwright `tests/fork/onboarding-practice.spec.ts` (26 tests: full path, slow starter with progress, late fill and the 90 s move on (fake clock), nothing heard, cancel, ⌃⌥ copy, celebration ± reduced motion, counter from 0, wpm and seconds-left math, early finish, languages, paste blocked, Try again, Recalculate, slider, every skip, summary and launch at login, Back, no model names, keyboard-only, RTL, axe light/dark for all five screens) and `onboarding.spec.ts` updated (shortcut test → Practice; shared backend `onboarding-fixture.ts`). **Not verified:** everything on a real Mac (queued: `scripts/wb-s29b/run.sh` + MAC-TEST-SCRIPT S-29b.2-.7): the practice take through the real hotkey and our own window in front, the queued path on a real slow download, typing in WebKit, the login item, the celebration by eye; the 10-person study S-29b.1 (§5.3, 0 stuck) needs people; VoiceOver by ear (S-37); 67 new strings are copy-English/`untranslated` in 25 locales pending the wave's machine batch.
- **Mac wave 2 fix (2026-11-03, `fix/wave2`; decided):** nothing registers a login item before the user chooses. F-07's `FIRST_RUN_DEFAULTS` no longer sets `autostart_enabled` (the first bundled release launch registered an SMAppService item before Welcome and macOS posted "Login Item Added"; quitting during onboarding left it). The Summary's "Open Wispr Bro when you log in" stays checked by default and is the only first-run writer, when "Start dictating" finishes setup. Installs that recorded the old default keep their current value (test `a_first_run_never_registers_a_login_item_and_an_existing_one_stays`). Queued: MAC-TEST-SCRIPT S-29a.7.

### S-30 First-use chooser, pill spotlight, setup checklist

- **Goal:** turn setup into a habit: a first real use and a next step.
- **User-visible outcome:** the main window opens with "What do you want to try first?" (Ask an AI assistant, Jot a note, Reply to a message, Write an email, Post a comment, Draft a document, Prompt a coding tool) with a captioned animated scene each → "Try it now" dims the screen and spotlights the bar with a hint ("Now open your email and hold fn") until the first real dictation in another app or Esc → home shows a "Use it in your own apps" banner. A setup checklist card sits in the sidebar (as in R1) and ticks itself, chaining to the next step. Items register through a checklist registry as their features exist: Try it in another app, Add a word, Create a snippet, Launch at login (here); Try a Transform (added by S-31); Try whispering (added by S-32).
- **R2 rows closed:** A-16, B-31, B-44
- **R4 parts:** driver.js 1.10.2 (MIT, adopt; `t()` strings only, never user text); Handy `Demo.tsx` scene engine (MIT, extend); own click-through dim NSPanel `fork-spotlight` (W-08).
- **New fork files:** `src/fork/onboarding/FirstUseChooser.tsx`, `src/fork/onboarding/scenes/*.tsx`, `fork/pill/spotlight.rs`, `src/fork/hub/{SetupChecklist,checklistRegistry}.tsx` (page `fork-spotlight` added to `src/fork/vite-inputs.ts`).
- **Tests:** checklist auto-ticks from events; registry adds items later without changes here; spotlight dismissal rules; Esc observed, not blocked.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** spotlight panel is click-through and sits above full-screen apps; dismisses on the first dictation in Mail.
- **Exit criteria:** every chooser option has a scene and a matching hint.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-29b, S-03, S-15a, S-16, S-17.
- **As built (2026-11-02), decisions that refine this block** (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "First use: chooser, bar spotlight, setup checklist (S-30)"; procedure in `docs/fork/MAC-TEST-SCRIPT.md` S-30, queued for the Mac wave): (1) **When the chooser shows:** once, when the Hub mounts after onboarding is `completed` with no `first_use_choice` and `first-use.chooser` not in `dismissed_banners`; Maybe later, ✕ and Esc store that id; the checklist's "Show me" and the banner's "Try something else" reopen it on the saved choice. Ids `ai_chat`, `note`, `message`, `email`, `comment`, `document`, `coding` (`firstUse.ts` ↔ `spotlight.rs`, parity test). The first option is preselected (no disabled state). (2) **Scenes:** one stage for all seven on a port of Handy's `Demo.tsx` beat-script engine (`scenes/engine.ts`, MIT): a drawn app window, the spoken words as a large caption lit word by word, the bar listening then transcribing, the clean text landing; content per choice in `fork.json` (our wording); Pause/Play (WCAG 2.2.2); reduced motion shows the finished frame; one `role="img"` whose name includes the written text. (3) **Spotlight** (`fork/pill/spotlight.rs`, own W-08 code): a `fork-spotlight` web view NSPanel on the bar's display at `NSMainMenuWindowLevel` (24, one below the bar's status level), all Spaces + full-screen auxiliary, `setIgnoresMouseEvents(true)`, non-activating; an SVG mask dim with a rounded cut-out (248 × 64 pt) around the bar and the hint card on the side away from the edge ("Next step · Open your email", how to dictate in fn or Control+Option wording, "Press Esc to close this hint"); it follows the bar across displays at 2 Hz and is destroyed when it ends. While it is on, the resting bar shows even with "Always show the bar" off or "Hide for an hour". **Ends on:** the first take pasted (`confirmed`/`unverified`) with another app in front (a take into Wispr Bro itself, e.g. the Scratchpad, does not count; practice takes never get there), Esc (observed through the S-01 registry, never blocked; an Esc that cancelled a take is the take's), Hide the hint in the Hub, or **10 minutes** (decided: a user who walked away should not come back to a dimmed screen). `fork_spotlight_start` answers `false` without a bar (not macOS / not a product runtime): the home banner says so. VoiceOver hears the hint. `WISPRBRO_S30_SPOTLIGHT=<choice>` starts it at launch on test identities only (Mac driver). (4) **Home:** "Use it in your own apps" replaces the promo banner until `try_in_app` ticks or it is dismissed: the hint and how, Show the hint again / Hide the hint (while on screen), Try something else. `Banner` gained `secondaryAction`. (5) **Checklist:** registry `registerChecklistItem({id, order, icon, titleKey, actionKey, bodyKey?, isDone?, refreshOn?, run, reveal?})` (`reveal` since S-32) in `checklistRegistry.tsx` (S-31/S-32 register from their own modules; `checklistRegistry.test.ts` shows a later item slots in by order); built-ins in `checklistItems.ts`: Try it in another app (10; ticked by the backend, `fork/onboarding/checklist.rs`, on the same pasted-take signal, so it ticks while the Hub is parked), Add a word (20; any word or replacement), Create a snippet (30), Open at login (40; Handy's `autostart_enabled`, one-click "Turn on"). Ticks persist in `onboarding.checklist_done` via `fork_checklist_mark` (idempotent, validated), so a deleted word stays ticked. `isDone` runs on mount, window focus and each item's events. **Chaining:** when the item started from the card ticks and the window is on screen, the next open item is _shown_ after 700 ms, never run: its optional `reveal` opens its page with its coach mark (Add a word → Snippets with its coach mark), and an item without one (Open at login, the lessons) gets its row pointed at in the card (`data-highlight`, 8 s or until clicked). `run` is for the user's click only, so finishing an earlier item can never turn on launch at login (changed in S-32, which closed S-31's finding); ticks from elsewhere only advance the card. Coach marks are driver.js 1.10.2 (adopted, `t()` strings only). The card hides with ✕ (`hub.checklist`), not in the collapsed sidebar, and says "All set" when done. No migration; schema stays v6; no Handy file (the `package.json` budget +1 for driver.js). **Tests:** Rust `fork::pill::spotlight` (cut-out bottom/top/secondary display, Esc rules, own-app take, timeout/stop/restart, unknown choice, hints exist for every choice + parity) and `fork::onboarding::checklist` (idempotent, validated, own app); `bun src/fork/hub/checklistRegistry.test.ts`; Playwright `tests/fork/first-use.spec.ts` (29 tests: chooser, scenes, keyboard-only, Esc, RTL, banner, checklist, chaining, coach mark, spotlight page, axe light/dark). Gates after the rebase onto S-02/S-26b/S-38 part 2: 1,580 Rust tests passed/33 ignored, fork clippy 0, 553 Playwright passed/10 skipped plus three that timed out only under load (passed alone; `openHub` now waits 20 s for the first Hub compile; two pill specs got 120 s budgets), guards, ESLint, tsc, translations, formatting. **Not verified:** everything on a real Mac (queued: `scripts/wb-s30/run.sh` + MAC-TEST-SCRIPT S-30.1-.6): click-through, the level over full-screen apps, Esc through the real tap, the end on a real Mail take, the hint by eye on two displays; VoiceOver by ear (S-37); new strings are copy-English/`untranslated` in 25 locales pending the wave's machine batch.
- **Mac wave 3 fix (2026-11-03, `fix/wave3`):** What's New no longer opens over the chooser (finding 4; see S-28); the spotlight panel keeps `canHide` off, like the bar's panels.

### Tutorials

### S-31 Lesson engine and Transforms tutorial

- **Goal:** every feature teaches itself with a short scene and a practice step.
- **User-visible outcome:** a generic lesson modal (progress dashes, close, back): intro scene → practice in a simulated app with success detection → "Turn on X". "How it works" opens animated scenes (no video). Transforms lesson: three cards (Original / Polish / Prompt Engineer) and a benefits ticker; "Select the text, then press ⌥1"; on success "Text rewritten. Press ⌥O to view the changes; ⌘Z undoes it." with a spotlight on the bar's "Done · View changes"; "Turn on Transforms". Lessons re-open from the Help popover's Essentials, page banners (the S-22 banner buttons appear now) and "Start tutorial" buttons. Adds "Try a Transform" to the setup checklist.
- **R2 rows closed:** B-39, B-40, B-45, D-43
- **Regression protected (HAVE):** B-24
- **R4 parts:** Handy `Demo.tsx` (MIT, extend); driver.js (adopt); SpeakoFlow `tourScenes.tsx` real-component tours (MIT, port idea).
- **New fork files:** `src/fork/tutorials/{LessonEngine,LessonModal,PracticeComposer,SuccessDetector,lessonRegistry}.tsx`, `src/fork/tutorials/lessons/transforms.ts`.
- **Tests:** lesson engine state machine; success detection with mocked events; re-entry points; checklist item registration.
- **Exit criteria:** the Transforms lesson completes end-to-end on a real Mac in under 2 min.
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-22, S-30.
- **As built (2026-11-03)** (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Lessons: engine and Transforms tutorial (S-31)"; procedure in `docs/fork/MAC-TEST-SCRIPT.md` S-31, queued for the Mac wave): `src/fork/tutorials/{LessonEngine.ts,lessonRegistry.tsx,LessonModal.tsx,PracticeComposer.tsx,SuccessDetector.tsx,scenes/,lessons/transforms.tsx}`, `fork/tutorials/practice.rs`, `fork/commands/tutorials.rs` (`fork_lesson_practice`, event `ForkLessonShortcut`), a guard line in the transform/View changes hotkey handlers (`fork/transforms/mod.rs`). No Handy file, no migration, no dependency. **Decisions:** (1) **Generic engine:** a lesson is data (`registerLesson`): a Try it list and an optional How it works list of steps that render themselves; `gated` practice steps continue after success or Skip (Skip is on by default, so no lesson dead-ends: S-32's criterion); the last How scene leads to "Try it yourself", Back crosses back; the last Try step's button is the feature's "Turn on X" (Done when already on). Registering wires Help › Essentials (S-26b `setHelpLessons`; lesson items open after the menu closed so the dialog keeps focus), the checklist item (S-30 registry) and `useLesson(id)` for page buttons. The R5 file names hold except `LessonEngine.ts` (pure, no JSX) and `lessons/transforms.tsx` (JSX). (2) **Practice is in the Hub, never a real paste:** the user selects text in a drawn chat (`PracticeComposer`, a real field) and presses _their own_ Polish chord (shown from settings; ⌃⌥1 if they switched). With Transforms off the key reaches the web view (keydown in the capture phase, the ⌥ character suppressed); with Transforms on the fork tap swallows it, so while the practice step is mounted `fork_lesson_practice` (120 s TTL, renewed every 60 s, off on unmount) makes the hotkey handlers emit `fork-lesson-shortcut` instead of running when Wispr Bro is frontmost (in any other app the shortcut runs as usual). This avoids AX reads and ⌘V into our own WebKit window (the S-20/S-24 Mac-wave findings). (3) **The rewrite is real when it can be:** `fork_transform_preview` with the user's own Polish (or the transform whose chord was pressed: Prompt Engineer, a custom one), 12 s cap; with no engine, an error or a timeout, a written example replaces the whole message and the page says it is an example (never claims a rewrite that did not happen). The result is inserted through the editing command, so the field's own ⌘Z undoes it as one step, matching the success text. (4) **Spotlight:** the drawn bar inside the lesson ("Done · View changes") is spotlighted with a dim over the simulated app and gets focus; View changes or the user's View changes chord shows the word diff (`fork_transform_diff`, with a local LCS fallback). The real screen-wide bar spotlight (S-30) is not used here: the practice run is not a recorded transform, so the real bar's View changes would show another run. (5) **Checklist:** "Try a Transform" (`try_transform`, order 35, between Create a snippet and Open at login) ticks when the lesson closes after its practice succeeded (not mid-lesson, so S-30's chaining never pulls the user out) or when any transform run is recorded (`fork_transform_latest`); a skipped practice does not tick it. (6) **How it works:** three drawn, looping scenes (select, press, see the changes with struck/inserted words) on a generic beat loop generalised from S-30's port of Handy's `Demo.tsx` (MIT); Pause/Play, reduced motion shows the finished frame; each scene is one `role="img"` with a description. (7) **Examples step:** three cards and a benefits ticker (CSS marquee, pauses on hover and with its own button, static wrapped list under reduced motion; the duplicate copy is `aria-hidden`). (8) **Entry points:** Transforms page banner Try it / How it works (they go with the banner when it is dismissed), the header's Start tutorial (always), Help › Essentials › Learn Transforms, the checklist. **Tests:** `bun src/fork/tutorials/LessonEngine.test.ts` (state machine), `bun src/fork/tutorials/SuccessDetector.test.ts` (chords), Rust `fork::tutorials::practice` (routing only while on with us in front, TTL, renew, off), Playwright `tests/fork/lessons.spec.ts` (17 tests: keyboard-only practice, ⌥O, undo step, no-selection hint, backend hand-over, Prompt Engineer, example fallback, Skip, Esc/Back, custom chord, How it works with Back, reduced motion, Start tutorial, Help, banner dismissal, checklist tick after close and from a real run, RTL, axe light/dark); S-22's spec now expects the banner buttons. Gates: see CONTEXT. **Not verified:** everything on a real Mac (queued: `scripts/wb-s31/run.sh` + MAC-TEST-SCRIPT S-31.1-.4, incl. the < 2 min exit criterion, the tap hand-over and a real engine rewrite); VoiceOver by ear (S-37); 68 new strings are copy-English/`untranslated` in 25 locales pending the wave's machine batch. **Found (S-30's area, fixed in S-32):** S-30's chaining ran the next item's `run` by itself, and Open at login's `run` turns autostart on, so finishing an earlier item from the card could enable launch at login without a click on it; chaining now only shows the next item (S-30 "As built").

### S-32 Whisper tutorial and microphone quiz

- **Goal:** Wispr's quiet-dictation flow with a concrete recommendation.
- **User-visible outcome:** home banner "Dictate quietly · Learn how" → (1) scene: speaking softly still works, (2) how to whisper with the built-in mic, (3) practice: whisper a short email in a simulated composer (Continue enables when ≥ 70 % of the words match; otherwise tips: get closer, raise input volume) → quiz: (1) "Pick a comfortable microphone" with our own illustrations of four mic types and a "More about mics" link to our docs page, (2) "Where do you usually work?" (private / mixed / shared spaces), (3) "Do you move between places?" (one desk / different places), (4) "Which microphones do you have?" → recommendation table (mic quality, whispering around others, whispering at home, price) with a "Best match" column; Done / Copy results. Adds "Try whispering" to the setup checklist.
- **R2 rows closed:** B-42, B-43
- **R4 parts:** lesson engine (S-31); own recommendation rules table.
- **New fork files:** `src/fork/tutorials/lessons/whisper.ts`, `src/fork/tutorials/MicQuiz.tsx`, `src/fork/tutorials/micTable.ts`, illustrations (own SVG), `docs/fork/user/microphones.md`.
- **Tests:** quiz → recommendation rules (all combinations); success threshold.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** whispered take on the starter and the full model at 30 cm.
- **Exit criteria:** the lesson never dead-ends (Skip always available).
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-31, S-08.
- **As built (2026-11-03)** (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Whisper lesson and microphone quiz (S-32)"; procedure in `docs/fork/MAC-TEST-SCRIPT.md` S-32, queued for the Mac wave): `src/fork/tutorials/lessons/{whisper.tsx,whisperMatch.ts}`, `src/fork/tutorials/{MicQuiz.tsx,micTable.ts}`, `src/fork/tutorials/art/MicArt.tsx`, `docs/fork/user/microphones.md`, `scripts/wb-s32/`; one Rust enum value (`ForkSettingsPane::SoundInput`). No Handy file, no migration, no dependency. **Decisions:** (1) **One lesson on S-31's engine**, eight Try it steps (no How it works list: the scene and the advice are the first two steps): the soft-voice scene, how to whisper with the built-in mic (about 30 cm, breath, a steady pace, let go last; our own drawing of the distance), the practice, the four questions, the table. The file is `lessons/whisper.tsx` (JSX), not `.ts`. (2) **The practice take is real and reuses S-29b's practice mode** (`fork_onboarding_practice`, renewed every 60 s, off when the step unmounts): a take started with Wispr Bro in front comes back as `fork-practice-take` into the drawn composer and is never pasted, kept out of the stats and never reaches the `try_in_app` tick (S-29b/S-30 rules, unchanged). No new backend path. (3) **The 70 % check lives in the whisper step** (`whisperMatch.ts`): the share of the sentence's words heard, without case, accents or punctuation, each heard word counted once, extra words free, per character for Han/kana; ≥ 70 % completes the gated step ("Heard you, even in a whisper"), below it the tips (closer, a firmer whisper, input volume) with **Open Sound settings** (Sound › Input; the new pane value, same local URL scheme as the bar's fix button) and the next take is graded again; an empty take says nothing came through. (4) **No speech model yet:** a queued take shows the sentence as a written example and says it is one (as S-31's rewrite example); it completes the step, because the user did whisper and the take waits in History; a late transcription replaces the example and is graded. (5) **Skip** is on the practice (S-31's gated default) and the four questions are not gated (Continue always on, an unanswered question weighs nothing), so the lesson never dead-ends (exit criterion, tested with no shortcut bound). (6) **Quiz and table** (`micTable.ts`, our own rules, no product names): four generic kinds (wired headset, desk/USB mic, wireless earbuds, built-in) rated Fair/Good/Great on mic quality, whispering around others, whispering at home, plus a price band ("You have one" when owned); one score from the answers (documented in the file and in `microphones.md`), a built-in mic never recommended to a Mac without one, ties to the earlier kind; "Best match" column and line; Copy results writes plain text (Wispr's Email results is cloud, B-43). Answers live for one opening of the lesson, never saved. "More about microphones" opens `docs/fork/user/microphones.md` on GitHub in the browser. (7) **Entry points:** the home promo banner's action "Dictate quietly: show me how" (C-29, S-15a's pending "Show me how"), Help › Essentials › Learn to dictate quietly (order 20, after Learn Transforms), checklist "Try whispering" (`try_whisper`, order 37: between Try a Transform 35 and Open at login 40), ticked when the lesson closes after the practice passed (or showed the example), not after Skip. (8) **S-30 chaining changed** (orchestrator decision): chaining calls an item's optional `reveal` (page + coach mark, no setting writes) or points at the next row; `run` only on a click; Add a word and Create a snippet reveal their pages, Open at login and the lessons have no `reveal`. S-30's "As built" and ARCHITECTURE updated. (9) **Mac acceptance driver** `scripts/wb-s32/run.sh`: a synthetic whisper (`say -o`, the Whisper voice when installed, scaled to −24 dB with a faint noise floor, `quiet_wav.py`) through the app's own `--transcribe-file` path on the starter and the full model, graded by `wordmatch.py` (mirrors `whisperMatch.ts`, parity checked by hand on four strings); no microphone, no speakers, nothing from `~/wb-bench`. **Tests:** `bun src/fork/tutorials/lessons/whisperMatch.test.ts` (threshold, normalisation), `bun src/fork/tutorials/micTable.test.ts` (all 960 answer combinations: one best match, never an absent built-in, never the desk mic for someone who moves, every kind reachable, named cases), Rust pane URL test extended, Playwright `tests/fork/whisper.spec.ts` (16 tests) plus `first-use.spec.ts` (six items; chaining points at a lesson's row and never runs it, no autostart call) and `help-share.spec.ts` (the new Help entry). Gates: 1,595 Rust tests passed/33 ignored, fork clippy 0, 592 Playwright passed/10 skipped (full fork suite on installed Chrome), ESLint, tsc, both translation checks, guards, formatting. **Not verified:** everything on a real Mac (queued: S-32.1 `scripts/wb-s32/run.sh all`, the whispered take on both models; S-32.2-.4 by a person: a live whispered take at 30 cm, the take outside, the drawings and links by eye); whether −24 dB is the right stand-in for a real whisper at 30 cm (S-32.2 checks it live); VoiceOver by ear (S-37); the new strings are copy-English/`untranslated` in 25 locales pending the wave's machine batch.

### S-34 Dictionary auto-learn (opt-in)

- **Goal:** learn names and jargon from the user's own corrections, with approval.
- **User-visible outcome:** toggle "Learn from my corrections" (off by default) in Dictionary and Data and Privacy (this slice adds the row to the S-27 page). After a paste, if the user fixes a word in that field (committed by Return, Tab or focus change within 30 s), the fixed word appears in Dictionary › Suggested with Accept / Reject. Rejected words never return.
- **R2 rows closed:** D-52
- **R4 parts:** BetterWispr `Integrations/CorrectionWatcher.swift`, `CorrectionLearner`, `Transcription/CorrectionReviewer.swift` + `CorrectionLearningTests` (GPL-3.0 VoiceInk-derived, port with attribution, W-27); VoiceInk `Features/Dictionary/AutoLearn/` `CorrectionDiffEngine` (GPL-3.0, port); TypeWhisper commit triggers (GPL, port idea).
- **New fork files:** `fork/dictionary/learn/{watcher,diff,review}.rs`.
- **Rules:** uses the S-05 lease; skips secure fields, terminals, password managers; stores only the corrected word pair, never field text.
- **Tests:** ported learning tests; multi-hunk diffs; reject list.
- **Real-Mac acceptance:** correct "Kubernetees" → "Kubernetes" in Slack; suggestion appears.
- **Exit criteria:** no field text persisted (checked in DB and logs).
- **Effort / depends on:** M / S-16, S-05, S-20, S-27, RS-04.
- **As built (2026-11-02):** `fork/dictionary/learn/{mod,watcher,diff,review,tests}.rs` (details in `docs/fork/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Dictionary auto-learn (S-34)"; ports in `docs/fork/PORTS.md`). After a take is pasted (fork macOS delivery, kind Take, not sent by an automatic Return) and with "Learn from my corrections" on, the field focused at the paste is armed on the S-05 AX thread (S-05 lease for Chromium/Electron, released at the end) after S-20's exclusions, then polled every 250 ms for at most 30 s. A commit is Return/keypad Enter (not with Shift or Option) or plain Tab from the fork tap (new commit-key observers on the S-01 registry), focus moving to another element or app, or the field emptying (a chat send). The dictation as it reads at the commit is found between 16-character anchors and diffed word by word; spelling fixes become suggestions in Dictionary › Suggested with Accept ✓ / Reject ✕; the toggle sits on that tab and on Settings › Data and Privacy (the S-27 row). No Handy file, no migration (S-16's schema already had `suggested`/`rejected`/`learn`), two new commands (`fork_dictionary_accept`, `fork_dictionary_reject`). **Decisions:** (1) learning only at a commit (R5), not when the text settles (BetterWispr); a key read too late (the composer already empty) uses the last value read; (2) the notes-model review (BetterWispr/VoiceInk `CorrectionReviewer`) is not ported: the user's approval replaces it (DEC-20), so field excerpts never reach any model; (3) an ASCII fix is suggested as a **word** (the recognizer vocabulary and Handy's fuzzy fix then catch other misspellings of it), a non-ASCII one as a **replacement** heard → corrected (Handy's fuzzy fix is ASCII-only, S-16); (4) a fix of an everyday word is dropped, not turned into a word (BetterWispr keeps it as vocabulary, but our words are fuzzy-applied); (5) "rejected never returns" covers rejected suggestions and deleted learned entries (both stay as `rejected` rows; Undo of a delete restores the row; adding the word by hand makes it active); anything the dictionary has in any state is not suggested again; (6) at most 5 suggestions per edit and 100 waiting; (7) `corrections` gets the pair (source `learn`, S-25's "most corrected word") when a suggestion is made and loses it on Reject; (8) no notification for a new suggestion (the Suggested count updates live); (9) erase (S-27) stops a watch first. **Exit criterion:** `learn::tests::only_the_word_pair_reaches_the_database` runs a watch over a field holding private text and dumps every text value of every `fork.db` table: only the pair is there; the `learn:` log lines take no text arguments (outcome, commit kind, counts, ms). The Mac driver (`scripts/wb-s34/`) checks the real files and log for the composer's sentinel. **Also fixed:** the shared `ui/Tabs` passed no `dir` to Radix, which reset every tab set (Dictionary, Insights, Style) to left-to-right inside the RTL Hub. **Tests:** ported BetterWispr learner, anchor, comparison and decision tests; multi-hunk diffs; the session's commit rules; commit keys; known/rejected/caps/accept/reject/delete-undo in a real `fork.db`; Playwright (toggle, accept/reject, full dictionary, keyboard-only, RTL, axe light/dark; Data and Privacy toggle persistence). Gates after the rebase onto `wispr-bro` (wave A landed): 1,468 Rust tests passed/32 ignored, fork clippy 0, fmt, Playwright fork suite 389 passed/10 skipped on installed Chrome (the Hub's every-page axe sweep timed out at 30 s once at load ~58 from parallel builders and passed alone in 9 s; it now has 180 s), ESLint, tsc, both translation checks, guards, `wb:format:check` and the bindings check. The eight new strings are copy-English/`untranslated` in 25 locales pending the wave batch. **Not run (queued for the S-19b + S-36 browser/AX Mac wave, MAC-TEST-SCRIPT S-34.1-.8):** the Kubernetees → Kubernetes take in a Slack stand-in in Chrome (Slack is not installed), Tab commit, rejected-never-returns, no-commit, the Card number exclusion, setting off, clipboard and the file/log leak check. **Not verified:** Slack itself and other Electron apps (lease path unit-tested only), a real person's typing rhythm against the 250 ms poll, VoiceOver by ear (S-37).

### S-35 Vocabulary importers: Wispr Flow and Handy (opt-in)

- **Goal:** one-click migration of the user's own words and snippets.
- **User-visible outcome:** in Dictionary and Snippets › Import: "From Wispr Flow" and "From Handy". The app copies the user's local Wispr Flow database (`~/Library/Application Support/Wispr Flow/flow.sqlite` + WAL) to a temp folder, checks the copy, opens it read-only, and previews dictionary words, replacements and snippets; Handy's `com.pais.handy/settings_store.json` custom words likewise. The user picks what to import.
- **R2 rows closed:** none directly (owner request, DEC-37); extends the import paths of C-64 (S-16) and C-71 (S-17)
